### [1] [Trapped fermions frustrate multiterminal Josephson networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23390)
*Antonio Manesco,Karsten Flensberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 多端约瑟夫森阵列中，奇宇称三结因俘获费米子产生相位绕转，形成长程阻挫相coherence；经典极限映射为Kagome XY反铁磁体，具有BKT转变与代数衰减的6e相位关联，表现为电流-相位关系中的三次谐波；量子相位涨落虽抑制6e相干，但通过自旋轨道耦合产生自旋关联与纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多端约瑟夫森阵列中由奇偶宇称引发的阻挫（parity-induced frustration），寻求超越常规2e超导相干（如4e、6e复合相干）的新机制，并研究俘获费米子之间的自旋纠缠。

Method: 聚焦奇宇称约瑟夫森三结：每个三结中俘获费米子诱导相位绕转，将多个三结连接后共享相位受限。经典区域下将阵列映射为Kagome XY反铁磁体，借助其BKT转变分析6e相位关联；并分析量子相位涨落及自旋轨道耦合的影响。

Result: 经典区域阵列对应Kagome XY反铁磁体，其BKT转变给出代数衰减的6e相位关联；6e相干表现为电流-相位关系中显著的第三次谐波。量子相位涨落抑制6e相干，但通过自旋轨道耦合可诱导俘获费米子间的自旋关联与纠缠。

Conclusion: 费米子诱导的阻挫可作为实现复合超导相干与相位介导自旋纠缠的途径，为超导-半导体人工量子材料提供新设计思路。

Abstract: Coherent control of fermions has become a standard approach for engineering quantum devices. Here, we explore parity-induced frustration in multiterminal Josephson arrays. We focus on odd-parity Josephson trijunctions, where the presence of a trapped fermion induces a phase winding in each trijunction. Connecting these trijunctions constrains their shared phases, resulting in a long-distance phase coherence where the frustrated configurations dominate the low-energy physics. In the classical regime, these arrays map onto the Kagome XY antiferromagnet, whose established Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) transition yields algebraically decaying $6e$ phase correlations. We show that the $6e$ phase coherence is manifested as a pronounced third harmonic in the current-phase relation of these arrays. While quantum phase fluctuations suppress the $6e$ coherence, we show that through spin-orbit coupling they also lead to spin correlations and entanglement between the trapped fermions. Our results establish fermion-induced frustration as a route to composite superconducting coherence and phase-mediated spin entanglement in superconductor-semiconductor artificial quantum materials.

</details>


### [2] [Real-Space Theory of Nonequilibrium Orbital Magnetization in Inhomogeneous Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23404)
*João M. Alendouro Pinho,Henrique P. Veiga,Alessandro Veneri,Daniel Passos,Ivo Souza,Bruno Amorim,João M. Viana Parente Lopes,Aires Ferreira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于无平移不变性体材料的实空间线性响应理论，描述直流电场诱导的轨道磁化；用单粒子格林函数及速度、位置算符表示线性于波矢的电导张量，从而给出规范不变的实空间公式，并在平移不变极限下回到Bloch图像；采用大规模Chebyshev展开实现，并在含空间非关联无序的Haldane模型上演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的轨道磁电效应理论主要建立在Bloch图像（平移不变晶体）之上，难以处理无序体系；同时非平衡轨道磁化在实空间中的规范不变表述尚缺，需要一种能同时覆盖完美晶体与无序体系的统一框架。

Method: 从对缓变电磁场的电流响应出发，推导非相互作用电子线性于波矢的电导张量，用单粒子格林函数以及速度、位置算符表达；据此构造长波长非平衡轨道磁化的规范不变实空间公式；数值上采用大规模Chebyshev展开实现。

Result: 在平移不变极限下，该形式化退化为已有的轨道磁电效应Bloch理论，验证了自洽性；在含空间非关联无序的Haldane模型上完成了大规模数值演示，展示了方法处理无序体系的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为无序及一般无平移不变体系中的电场诱导轨道磁化提供了规范不变的实空间线性响应理论，并与晶体极限的既有结果一致；配合Chebyshev大规模算法，可作为研究无序轨道磁电效应的实用工具。

Abstract: We put forward a real-space linear-response theory of orbital magnetization induced by a dc electric field in bulk materials lacking translation invariance. Starting from the current response to a slowly varying electromagnetic field, we show that the linear-in-wavevector conductivity tensor of non-interacting electrons in terms of single-particle Green's functions and the velocity and position operators. This provides a gauge-invariant real-space formulation of the long-wavelength non-equilibrium orbital magnetization of both perfect crystals and disordered systems. In the translationally invariant limit, the formalism recovers the established Bloch picture of orbital magneto-electric effects. We implement the formalism using large-scale Chebyshev expansions and illustrate its capabilities on a Haldane model in the presence of spatially uncorrelated disorder.

</details>


### [3] [Towards Defect Engineering of Orbital Magnetoelectric Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23405)
*João M. Alendouro Pinho,Henrique P. Veiga,Alessandro Veneri,Daniel Passos,Ivo Souza,Bruno Amorim,João M. Viana Parente Lopes,Aires Ferreira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文利用实空间框架研究金属轨道反铁磁体中由点缺陷诱导的电荷-轨道转换，发现缺陷强烈增强时间奇磁电效应、激活原本禁戒的时间偶响应，并产生纳米尺度轨道磁化热点甚至使净轨道矩反号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡态轨道磁化已被充分研究，但光场、电场等外场驱动下的非平衡轨道磁性理解仍很初步；同时传统Bloch能带图像难以描述缺陷等实空间不均匀性对磁电响应的影响。

Method: 采用新颖的实空间框架，超越常规Bloch能带图像，在金属轨道反铁磁体中分析点缺陷引起的电荷-轨道转换；并引入局域标记量实现非平衡态轨道磁化的空间分辨成像。

Result: 常见点缺陷显著增强时间奇磁电效应；依其局域对称性还可激活原本禁戒的时间偶响应；实空间图揭示缺陷形成纳米尺度轨道磁化热点，深刻改变体磁电响应，包括净轨道矩的反号。

Conclusion: 点缺陷与杂质可在原子尺度精确调控线性响应轨道矩，为轨道磁性的缺陷工程提供了可能。

Abstract: Orbital magnetization is a fundamental measure of time-reversal symmetry breaking in solids that is accessible by experiments. However, unlike the well-studied equilibrium case, the understanding of orbital magnetism resulting from external stimuli such as light and electric fields is still in its infancy. Here we employ a novel real-space framework to investigate charge-to-orbital conversion in a metallic orbital antiferromagnet beyond the conventional Bloch-band picture. We find that common point defects give rise to strongly enhanced time-odd magnetoelectric effects and, depending on their local symmetry, further activate otherwise-forbidden time-even responses. Moreover, we introduce a local marker that provides spatially resolved information on the orbital magnetization carried by non-equilibrium states. Real-space maps reveal that point defects create nanoscale hotspots of orbital magnetization leading to a profound modification of the bulk magnetoelectric response, including a sign reversal of the net orbital moment. Our findings may open up the possibility of engineering linear-response orbital moments with atomic-scale precision by point defects and impurities.

</details>


### [4] [Efficient interlayer interactions in non-planar molecule/transition-metal dichalcogenide two-dimensional heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23435)
*Johann Alvarez,Kamal Harrison,John Koptur-Palenchar,Miguel Gakiya-Teruya,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Michael Shatruk,Xiao-Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究离散非平面自旋交叉分子[Fe(tBu2qsal)2]（FTBQS）与二维TMD及hBN间隔层的层间相互作用，发现存在长作用距离的高效Förster共振能量转移与由能带对齐导致的电荷转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子与二维原子材料杂化器件的研究多集中于平面分子；受配体三维取向所限，将非平面分子整合进二维异质结仍具挑战。

Method: FTBQS具有块体分子晶体层、可机械剥离，从而实现洁净杂化异质结的确定性组装；结合荧光光谱与栅压调控光谱研究其与单层TMD、hBN间隔层的层间耦合。

Result: 即使插入hBN间隔层仍出现强烈PL猝灭，表明存在作用距离较长的有效Förster共振能量转移；栅压依赖光谱通过TMD层掺杂位移识别出源于TMD/FTBQS能带对齐的电荷转移。

Conclusion: 研究证实非平面离散分子与二维晶体之间可建立高效层间相互作用，为杂化电学、光学与自旋电子器件提供基础。

Abstract: Integration of molecules and two-dimensional (2D) atomic materials offer new opportunities for hybrid electronic devices. Most progress has focused on interfacing planar molecule. In contrast, incorporating non-planar molecules into 2D heterostructures remains challenging due to the three-dimensional orientations of ligands. Here we investigate interlayer interactions between discrete non-planar spin-crossover (SCO) molecules, [Fe(tBu2qsal)2] (FTBQS), monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs), and hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) spacers through optical spectroscopies. FTBQS features bulk molecular crystal layers and can be mechanically exfoliated, enabling deterministic assembly of clean hybrid heterostructures. Robust photoluminescence (PL) quenching, even with an hBN spacer, indicates efficient Förster resonance energy transfer with a long decay distance. Gate-dependent optical spectroscopy further identifies charge transfer from the doping shifts in the TMD layers due to the TMD/FTBQS electronic band alignment. Our results establish efficient interlayer interactions between non-plana, discrete molecules and 2D crystals for hybrid electrical, optical, and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [5] [Two-Dimensional Ferromagnetic Weyl Half-Semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23688)
*Yu-Yang Zhao,Chen-Hao Lv,Qing-Bo Liu,Wen-Neng Zhao,Xin-Yi Chen,Lun Xiong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理与蒙特卡罗模拟，预测单层BaMn₄O₈为动力学、热学、力学稳定的铁磁外尔半半金属，居里温度约1446 K，具完全自旋极化外尔点与费米弧且抗自旋轨道耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维铁磁拓扑材料是实现自旋极化拓扑态的理想平台，但外尔费米子与稳健高温铁磁性的共存仍难以实现，本文旨在寻找这一兼具二者的候选体系。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算结合蒙特卡罗模拟（20×20×1各向异性格点，有限尺寸标度估算转变温度）；通过自旋分辨电子结构与(100)边界表面态计算判定拓扑性质，并考察自旋轨道耦合的影响；同时验证动力学、热学与力学稳定性。

Result: 单层BaMn₄O₈呈完全自旋极化态：自旋向上通道在费米能级附近存在线性外尔能带交叉，自旋向下通道保持绝缘；(100)边界上可清晰观察到连接投影外尔点的费米弧；外尔点及费米弧在引入自旋轨道耦合后依然稳健；有限尺寸蒙特卡罗估计转变温度约1446 K。

Conclusion: 单层BaMn₄O₈是兼具高温铁磁性与完全自旋极化拓扑态的外尔半半金属候选材料，适用于自旋极化拓扑输运。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) ferromagnetic topological materials provide appealing platforms for realizing spin-polarized topological states, yet the coexistence of Weyl fermions and robust high-temperature ferromagnetism remains elusive. Here, based on first-principles calculations and Monte Carlo simulations, we predict monolayer BaMn$_4$O$_8$ to be a dynamically, thermally, and mechanically stable ferromagnetic Weyl half-semimetal. \textcolor{blue}{An anisotropic Monte Carlo simulation on a $20\times20\times1$ lattice yields a finite-size transition estimate of approximately 1446~K.} The spin-resolved electronic structure reveals a fully spin-polarized state, in which the spin-up channel hosts linear Weyl band crossings near the Fermi level, whereas the spin-down channel remains insulating. Corresponding Fermi-arc edge states connecting the projected Weyl points (WPs) are clearly observed along the (100) boundary, confirming the nontrivial topology. Importantly, both the Weyl points and their associated Fermi arcs remain robust in the presence of spin--orbit coupling (SOC). \textcolor{blue}{These results identify monolayer BaMn$_4$O$_8$ as a candidate for fully spin-polarized topological transport.

</details>


### [6] [Breaking the Myth of Four-Tier Stacked Transistors for Logic: an Investigation from Process to Device and Block-Level PPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23806)
*Jianxiang Jin,Haoran Lu,Yihan Luo,Yanbang Chu,Yibo Zhang,Jingru Jiang,Yuji Cheng,Wanyue Peng,Xun Jiang,Feiyu Teng,Kairang Guo,Ming Li,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang,Heng Wu,Ru Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作首次在2A节点设计规则下评估4层堆叠晶体管（CFFET）能否将密度增益转化为芯片级PPA收益，结论是单纯垂直堆叠不足以保证足够的PPA优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4层堆叠晶体管理论上可带来显著的单元高度与密度增益（名义单元高度降低约40%），但此前缺乏对这类工艺能否在真实芯片级（标准单元、局部互连、RO、物理实现）转化为实际PPA收益的系统性评估。

Method: 提出实用的双面4层晶体管堆叠CFFET（Flipped CFET-to-CFET）工艺；在2A节点规则下构建涵盖标准单元设计、局部互连、环形振荡器分析与物理实现的DTCO框架；与2层堆叠的FFET对标；提出类似俄罗斯方块的单元拼接（Tetris-like cell stitching）以支持非矩形单元、降低面积开销；并受既有工作启发提出优化的NanoStack CFFET。

Result: CFFET最优配置（Symmetric Power）因复杂的中段（MOL）寄生，RO频率仍比FFET低11.2%；块级上Ibex RISC-V核面积仅减少12.8%（远低于40%名义单元高度缩减），等功耗下速度慢10.9%；Tetris-like单元拼接在1-bit ALU中回收7.4%面积。

Conclusion: 直接的垂直4层堆叠不足以保证足够的PPA收益，MOL寄生与块级实现开销是主要瓶颈；所提出的单元拼接与NanoStack优化及设计洞见可为未来先进架构提供指导。

Abstract: For the first time, this work evaluated whether 4-tier stacked transistors can translate the straightforward density gains into realistic chip-level scaling benefits. A practical dual-sided 4-tier transistor-stacking CFFET (Flipped CFET-to-CFET) process was proposed, on which a DTCO framework spanning standard-cell design, local interconnects, RO analysis, and physical implementation was built under 2A node design rules. The CFFET was also benchmarked with its 2-tier transistor-stacking counterparts FFET. Among CFFET configurations, the best-performing Symmetric Power still lags FFET by 11.2% in RO frequency due to the complex MOL parasitics. At the block level, CFFET reduces the Ibex RISC-V core area by only 12.8% (far below the 40% nominal cell-height reduction), while running 10.9% slower at iso-power. We further introduced a novel Tetris-like cell stitching to minimize the area overhead, which allows non-rectangular cell design and recovers 7.4% area in a 1-bit ALU design. An optimized NanoStack CFFET was finally proposed, inspired by previous work. This work indicates that straightforward vertical 4-tier stacking proves insufficient to guarantee enough PPA benefits, though the resulting design insights may guide future advanced architectures.

</details>


### [7] [Non-Abelian Hall Effects of Flavor-Waves in $\mathbb{CP}^{2}$ Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24134)
*Carlos Saji,Eduardo Saavedra,Alvaro S. Nunez,Roberto E. Troncoso*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在二维三角格子spin-1磁体中，以SU(3)相干态框架将偶极与四极涨落描述为两个耦合玻色子味，建立Bogoliubov–de Gennes问题，研究孤立与晶态CP²斯格明子的集体激发、非阿贝尔规范结构与拓扑输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CP²斯格明子携带偶极与四极（多极）自由度，其纹理混合两种自旋翻转激发，需要理解投影磁性纹理如何把多极激发与非阿贝尔输运、拓扑能带现象耦合起来。

Method: 采用SU(3)相干态表示，把磁偶极与四极矩涨落映射为两个耦合玻色子味（均匀极化态下对应单/双自旋翻转）；对非均匀CP²纹理构建玻色Bogoliubov–de Gennes方程，并分析支配味波传播的矩阵值规范联络；对数值弛豫得到的斯格明子晶体计算集体模式能带与Chern数，进而求热霍尔响应。

Result: 孤立斯格明子谱中包含局域模式，散射动力学表现出横向偏转并伴随同位旋进动；斯格明子晶体的集体模式能带具有非零Chern数，并据此给出相应的热霍尔响应。

Conclusion: 结果说明投影磁性纹理可将多极激发与非阿贝尔输运及拓扑能带效应耦合，为多极拓扑激发的研究提供框架。

Abstract: We investigate the collective excitations of isolated and crystalline $\mathbb{CP}^{2}$ skyrmions in a two-dimensional triangular-lattice spin-1 magnet. Within an $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ coherent-state framework, fluctuations of the magnetic dipole and quadrupole moments are described by two coupled bosonic flavors. In a uniform spin-polarized state, these flavors can be identified with single-spin-flip and double-spin-flip excitations, whereas a nonuniform $\mathbb{CP}^{2}$ texture mixes their character. We formulate the corresponding bosonic Bogoliubov--de Gennes problem and examine the matrix-valued gauge connection governing flavor-wave propagation. For isolated skyrmions, the calculated spectrum contains localized modes, and the scattering dynamics exhibit transverse deflection accompanied by isospin precession. For a numerically relaxed skyrmion crystal, we obtain collective-mode bands with nonzero Chern numbers and calculate the associated thermal Hall response. These findings illustrate how projective magnetic textures couple multipolar excitations to non-Abelian transport and topological band phenomena.

</details>


### [8] [Spin dynamics in disordered systems with generic spin-momentum couplings: A matrix-rational formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24346)
*Masataka Kakoi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种基于矩阵有理近似的微观框架，用于各向异性、非线性自旋-动量耦合赝自旋-1/2体系的无序平均密度矩阵动力学，可统一描述超快自旋动力学与长时弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散子（Diffuson）描述依赖低频或弱耦合展开，难以覆盖强耦合及非扩散区间的自旋动力学，需要一种不依赖这些展开的微观方法。

Method: 从Diffuson出发，对其频率依赖引入矩阵值有理近似（不依赖低频/弱耦合展开）；将极点方程改写为二次特征值问题，据此构造无序平均密度矩阵的实时演化。应用于Rashba+线性/立方Dresselhaus二维电子气、含Rashba耦合的d波交错磁体、三维双Weyl模型。

Result: 与直接数值模拟相比，在宽耦合强度范围内，淬火后自旋动力学与自旋弛豫时间均定量吻合，且超出扩散区。

Conclusion: 矩阵有理形式为超快自旋动力学与长时弛豫提供统一描述，可推广至多种自旋-动量耦合体系。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic framework for the disorder-averaged density-matrix dynamics of pseudospin-$1/2$ systems with anisotropic and nonlinear spin-momentum couplings. Starting from the Diffuson, we introduce a matrix-valued rational approximation for its frequency dependence that does not rely on a low-frequency or weak-coupling expansion. The resulting pole equation is recast as a quadratic eigenvalue problem, from which the real-time evolution of the disorder-averaged density matrix is constructed. We apply the formulation to a two-dimensional electron gas with Rashba and linear and cubic Dresselhaus couplings, a $d$-wave altermagnet with Rashba coupling, and a three-dimensional double-Weyl model. Comparisons with direct numerical simulations demonstrate quantitative agreement for both the post-quench spin dynamics and spin relaxation times across a broad range of coupling strengths, extending beyond the diffusive regime. The matrix-rational formulation provides a unified description of ultrafast spin dynamics and long-time relaxation.

</details>


### [9] [Spontaneous emission of atomic systems near metallic and semiconducting atomic chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24358)
*Abhishek Ghosh,David Dams,Antton Babaze,Garnett Bryant,Carsten Rockstuhl,Andrés Ayuela,Karolina Słowik,Marta Pelc*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了SSH模型有限原子链附近量子发射体的自发辐射，通过二聚化调控金属-半导体转变来分析电子结构如何决定自发辐射增强的强度、带宽与空间结构，并推广到反式聚乙炔与石墨烯纳米带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate the spontaneous emission of a quantum emitter placed near finite atomic chains described by the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model. By tuning the chain from a metallic to a semiconducting character through dimerization, we analyze how the electronic structure of an atomic-scale scatterer, together with the illumination profile, determines the strength, bandwidth, and spatial structure of the spontaneous emission enhancement. The resonant enhancement depends on both spectral matching and the emitter position relative to the induced charge and field patterns. In metallic chains, the nearly harmonic energy spectrum leads to overlapping optical transitions and strong resonances. For semiconducting chains, dimerization opens a gap and leads to a modified density of electronic states, redistributing the response over a broader spectral range and reducing its peak intensity. Coulomb interactions affect the optical resonances through blueshift or modification of their spatial character, and act differently on collective and single-particle-like excitations. Finally, we show that the same mechanisms demonstrated in atomic chains persist in realistic quasi-one-dimensional systems, including trans-polyacetylene chains and graphene nanoribbons. Our findings indicate a general direction for engineering light-matter interactions at the atomic scale, and may offer design principles for tunable nanoscale photonic and quantum-optical devices.

We show that a nonsymmorphic momentum-space action is possible only when the unitary translation subgroup is combined with rotations about a common axis for a coplanar or noncoplanar spin space group of spin-$1/2$ systems. Its factor system originates entirely from the double cover $\mathrm{Spin}(2)\to SO(2)$, yielding a general formula for the fractional reciprocal-lattice translations and hence for the point-group action on the Brillouin zone. We further prove that a momentum-space nonsymmorphic group is realizable by a spin space group if and only if its Brillouin-zone action contains a two-point orbit. Abstract: First-order phase transitions are governed by the balance between zero-temperature energy, thermal vibrational energy, and entropy, yet these contributions are rarely accessible experimentally. Here, we establish a diffraction-based route to determine them for the prototypical Si(111)-In atomic-wire system. From the temperature-dependent Debye-Waller attenuation of reflection high-energy electron diffraction (RHEED) intensities we extract distinct Debye temperatures for the low-temperature $(8{\times}2)$ ground state and the high-temperature $(4{\times}1)$ phase. The softer vibrational spectrum of the high-temperature phase yields the entropy gain that stabilizes this phase above the critical temperature $T_\mathrm{c}$. Evaluating the thermodynamic balance between internal energy and entropy at $T_\mathrm{c} = 125\,\mathrm{K}$ yields a zero-temperature energy difference of $ΔE_0 = 138\,\mathrm{meV}/(8{\times}2)$, including the difference in vibrational zero-point energies of the two phases, thus providing direct access to hidden thermodynamic parameters of a first-order surface phase transition.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In 1986 Bapat and Sunder asked whether $\operatorname{per}(A)$ is the largest eigenvalue of $F_A=[a_{i,j}\operatorname{per}(A(i,j))]$ for every positive semidefinite Hermitian $A$, where $A(i,j)$ deletes row $i$ and column $j$. Drury disproved this over the complex field in 2018 and conjectured that the property holds for real positive semidefinite matrices. We disprove Drury's conjecture with a rank-$4$, $32\times32$ integer Gram matrix and an integer vector whose Rayleigh quotient exceeds $\operatorname{per}(A)$; verification uses integer arithmetic. Together, the two counterexamples resolve the Bapat-Sunder eigenvalue conjecture negatively over both fields. Counterexamples exist of every order $n\ge32$. As an application to quantum optics, the violation yields a $36$-mode orthogonal interferometer and a direction of relative arrival-time delays among $32$ photons with identical-variance Gaussian wave packets along which every sufficiently small nonzero delay increases the probability that all photons are detected within four designated output modes.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLQC的多数应用依赖于对其所需纠缠的下界理解，但何种信道性质导致纠缠成本一直不清楚，已有的下界大多只适用于特殊构造的例子，缺乏一般性、忠实的下界工具。

Abstract: In a non-local quantum computation (NLQC), shared entanglement along with a single simultaneous round of quantum communication is used to implement a joint channel. NLQC has many applications, most of which benefit from an understanding of lower bounds on the entanglement needed to implement a given channel as an NLQC. However, the properties of a channel that lead to it having an entanglement cost in NLQC have remained unclear, with most lower bounds only applying to specially constructed examples. Here, we give a new lower bound technique for unitaries based on scrambling: we find that a unitary has zero entanglement cost if and only if it does not scramble. Further, we find that the tripartite mutual information, an entropic object that quantifies the degree of scrambling, provides a quantitative lower bound on entanglement cost in NLQC. This is the first known lower bound on entanglement cost for unitaries in NLQC which is faithful. Our results also relate scrambling and NLQC for the first time. Leveraging the faithful scrambling based bound, we then show that the controllable correlation, an earlier studied quantity, is also a faithful lower bound, resolving an earlier conjecture of Cleve and May.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规直觉认为无法随时间调制哈密顿量时系统只能沿固定轨迹演化，控制能力有限；作者希望定量检验这一直觉，明确时不变哈密顿量究竟能实现多少态制备能力。

Abstract: How much can be prepared with a quantum system whose Hamiltonian cannot be modulated in time? Common intuition suggests that the answer is not much. Without a knob to turn during the evolution, a system seems confined to a fixed trajectory through its state space, while time-dependent control can steer it almost anywhere. We put this intuition to the test by studying autonomous control, i.e., state preparation with a time-invariant Hamiltonian, where the only freedom lies in the static coupling strengths and the evolution time. We characterize basic mathematical properties of the autonomously reachable set and introduce two criteria of unreachability: a certificate that is cheap to evaluate and rigorously excludes that a target state can be reached for any choice of control parameters, and a stronger criterion based on exhaustive numerical optimization. With these tools we estimate the probability that a typical state in Hilbert space is unreachable under randomly drawn control terms. The dimensionality of the reachable set turns out to be independent of the system, yet the fraction of Hilbert space lying close to it depends strongly on the choice of control Hamiltonians. Strikingly, for sufficiently random controls and long enough evolution times, almost every state can become approximately reachable, even with the fewest generators that permit full controllability with time-dependent couplings. Autonomous control can approach the expressivity of time-dependent control, at the cost of longer evolution times.

</details>

We show that this speedup is offset by relaxation of the additional excited qubits, so the single-qubit coupling and lifetime still set the coherence requirement. Transferring exactly one excitation also requires an anharmonic mechanical mode, which an auxiliary qubit coupled to the mechanics can provide. Abstract: Ground-state energy estimation (GSEE) and ground-state preparation (GSP) are fundamental problems in quantum computing. Given a Hamiltonian $H$ with $\|H\|\le1$ and a guiding state $|ψ\rangle$ with overlap at least $γ>0$ with the ground space of $H$, the goal in GSEE is to estimate the ground-state energy of $H$ to additive error $δ$ with failure probability at most $\varepsilon$, while in GSP it is to prepare a state with ground-space weight at least $1-\varepsilon$, given a gap lower bound $Δ$. Their costs are measured by quantum query complexity, i.e., the number of oracle calls needed to access $H$. However, these models do not directly account for the sparsity of $H$, measured by the maximum number $s$ of nonzero entries in any row or column. In the sparse-access model, oracles instead return the locations and values of these entries. This introduces the additional parameter $s$, and it remained open how query complexity depends jointly on $s$, $γ$, $δ$ or $Δ$, and $\varepsilon$. We resolve this by proving sparse-query lower bounds $Ω(\sqrt{s}\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γδ))$ for GSEE and $Ω(\sqrt{s}\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γΔ))$ for GSP, even for stoquastic Hamiltonians. We also show that these constructions recover the earlier time-evolution and block-encoding lower-bound scalings. We then give matching upper-bound algorithms in the reversible in-place sparse-location model. Thus, we obtain $Θ(\sqrt{s}\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γδ))$ for GSEE and $Θ(\sqrt{s}\log(1/\varepsilon)/(γΔ))$ for GSP, showing that $\sqrt{s}$ is necessary and sufficient and completing the joint dependence on all problem parameters.

</details>

Spreading an encoded coordinate over several qumodes lowers the average occupation number of each mode, and adding counterterms to the Hamiltonian reduces the required squeezing amount. For more complex molecules, each additional nuclear coordinate is represented directly as a single qumode or an encoded register using the same native operations, so the framework extends naturally to larger systems.

Abstract: Monge-Kantorovich optimal transport is a mathematical framework that determines the most efficient way to transform one probability distribution into another while minimizing a given cost function. Its central constructions include the Wasserstein distances, which quantify the space of probability distributions, and dual formulations that link to convex analysis. Its standard quantum generalization replaces probability distributions with density operators and cost function with positive semidefinite cost operator, which should be regarded as spatial quantum optimal transport. In this work, we propose the quantum optimal transport problem in spacetime through the spatiotemporal phase-space Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distribution and spatiotemporal states and through spatiotemporal state. In the spatiotemporal state formalism, the cost operators are generally non-Hermitian, and the spatiotemporal couplings are generally non-Hermitian and yield the correct reduced local states. The Margenau-Hill phase-space distribution gives rise to a real-valued optimal transport in spatiotemporal phase space; in the spatiotemporal state formalism, this corresponds to a Hermitian spatiotemporal coupling state. We also introduce a spatiotemporal quantum Wasserstein metric based on a metric in spatiotemporal phase spaces and show that it satisfies the metric axioms.

Abstract: Quantum charge-coupled device (QCCD) architectures enable scalable trapped-ion quantum computing by dynamically shuttling ions between specialized processing and storage zones. We present a co-design framework built on one Lean-verified compiler. The hardware description and its operating rules are compiler inputs, so a single theorem covers every candidate design: the target code of every compilation preserves its program's behavior and obeys the hardware rules. Across 75 benchmark programs of 11 to 130 qubits, the best design found takes 66% less modeled execution time on average than the worst design of equal capacity. Abstract: Quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) implements matrix inversion using bounded polynomials. Increasing polynomial degree can improve normalization and reduce amplified query cost, with savings that depend on the error criterion. However, existing bounded methods either fix the normalization or work far below the condition numbers of fault-tolerant resource estimates. We therefore analyze this trade-off for absolute reciprocal error, which controls inverse-operator accuracy, and for residual error, which suffices for normalized linear solves. For absolute reciprocal error, we establish a scaling limit to a constrained least-norm problem for entire functions and characterize its high-accuracy frontier using elliptic integrals. Constructions built from near-optimal limiting functions achieve certified query-cost savings over the rescaled minimax baseline of at least 11.8 percent at condition number ten thousand with error one millionth, and at least 22.8 percent at one million with error one ten-billionth. A simpler two-term rule also has certified savings, and its phase factors at condition number ten thousand pass numerical checks at selected points. For residual error, a sparse Chebyshev construction achieves certified savings over the residual-minimax baseline of at least 8.7 percent at condition number ten thousand with normalized-state error at most one hundred-millionth, and at least 13.1 percent at one million with error at most one ten-billionth. These results quantify the saving that a joint choice of degree and normalization gives beyond the minimax baselines.

Abstract: The Monte Carlo sign problem poses a fundamental obstacle to classical simulation of quantum many-body systems, yet its connection to quantum resources remains poorly understood. We probe this connection by systematically tuning the degree of nonstoquasticity in random k-local Hamiltonians. In numerical tensor-network studies, we find the sign complexity and the quantum resources of entanglement and nonstabilizerness of the ground state to be strongly suppressed in a broad regime up to substantial nonstoquasticity. As nonstoquasticity is further increased, the considered quantum resources experience sharp crossovers into a regime where they are strongly enhanced. Surprisingly, numerical quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations drastically deteriorate in the same region, even though the Hamiltonian is already considerably nonstoquastic well before it, indicating that the failure of ground-state QMC is correlated more with the resource content of the state than the sign structure of the Hamiltonian. We corroborate these findings analytically by constructing an approximate ground state of k-local Hamiltonians in the stoquastic limit. Moreover, we identify a distinct behaviour in graph states. Their nonstabilizerness always vanishes, but, as we demonstrate analytically, on bipartite graphs the sign can be easily cured while in multipartite graph states it cannot. Our results thus shed light on the highly subtle interplay between quantum resources and the computational difficulty encountered by QMC methods.

Abstract: High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes could greatly reduce the qubit count of silicon spin-qubit processors, but their checks are long-range and of high weight. Shuttling one ancilla electron through every data qubit of a check measures such checks, but a single fault on the ancilla spreads to several data qubits, so the circuit distance can fall far below the code distance. For codes of distance 8 to 12 the flag raises the circuit distance by one to four, and the Gross code [[144,12,12]] rises from 7 to 10. BP+OSD does not reach the full circuit distance of the flagged circuits, so our flagged logical error rates are upper bounds: decoding the same shots exactly raises the flag's gain on the distance-6 codes from 3-7x to 5-17x for the weight-6 BB code, and from 1-12x to 5-90x at weights 8 and 10 for p >= 2.5x10^-4.

Abstract: Neutrosophic annotation frameworks for quantum measurement attach an external triple $(t,i,f)$ to each Born probability, and explicitly leave open how indeterminacy could be defined operationally. The normalized Gini index of the outcome splits as $4TF = \mathcal{I}_ρ(A) + 4 \mathrm{Var}_ρ(A)$, a law of total variance that separates the instrument contribution from the state contribution; the identity is a special case of Massar's POVM uncertainty operator, and our contribution is its neutrosophic reading. Abstract: Near a Liouvillian exceptional point, spectral perturbation theory becomes ill-conditioned, yet the observable dynamics of a finite-dimensional open system remains regular in the perturbation. We derive scaling laws showing that nonperipheral Jordan blocks raise the degree of the polynomial-in-time factors multiplying decaying exponentials, which are present for any generator as resonances, and we quantify this excess. We further establish a uniform-in-time error estimate for the perturbative series, valid for any relaxing finite-dimensional Lindbladian and controlled by a parameter $q = |\varepsilon|R$, where $\varepsilon$ is the perturbation strength and $R$ is a constant built from unperturbed data. Abstract: The fractured low-energy landscape of spin glasses presents a notorious barrier to optimization. Here, we treat the QAOA distribution as a statistical-physics object and probe its geometry directly in the thermodynamic limit. We find that typical QAOA samples remain macroscopically uncorrelated, even as their sensitivity to this coupling grows with circuit depth $p$. By contrast, the low-energy tail shows a much stronger tendency to overlap and reveals a Parisi-like phase with the same critical behavior as the thermal spin-glass transition. The onset of this organization moves toward the typical output as the circuit deepens, consistent with the separation vanishing only as $p\to\infty$. Together, these findings suggest a route distinct from structure-exploiting classical algorithms and thermal sampling, one in which the QAOA approaches the Parisi energy before Parisi geometry becomes typical.

Abstract: Quantum memories offer to significantly extend the performance of quantum networks, but their complexity and limited performance at telecommunication wavelengths have so far hindered practical deployment. Abstract: Resonantly driven two-level quantum batteries (QBs) suffer from strong pulse-area dependence and energy backflow inherent to Rabi oscillations. To address this limitation, we propose charging of a two-level QB with chirped pulses. We investigate four charging protocols formed by pairing hyperbolic secant (sech) or Gaussian pulse envelopes with linear or hyperbolic-tangent (tanh) frequency sweeps. We analyze the underlying adiabatic dynamics and derive the criteria for robust chirped charging, which incorporate the sweep bandwidth, nonadiabatic transitions, and dissipative effects. Under strong chirp modulation, the final stored energy and ergotropy converge to stable plateaus that are nearly insensitive to the pulse area across an extended parameter window. Of the four schemes, the sech pulse with tanh chirp delivers the widest ergotropy plateau and the weakest pulse area sensitivity. Compared with counterdiabatic driving, the sech tanh protocol attains stable charging performance without demanding an extra drive quadrature. Abstract: Certifying quantum non-classicality like Wigner negativity typically demands full optical state tomography. This work introduces a noise-resilient framework for witnessing optical Wigner negativity using phase-resolved quantum photon-induced near-field electron microscopy (QPINEM). A post-measurement witness matrix is constructed via Bochner's theorem from recorded electron spectra, where negative eigenvalues certify non-classicality. To ensure reliability under realistic experimental conditions, a noise-aware selection procedure decouples discovery from statistical validation. Benchmarked across various optical preparations-including vacuum-Fock superpositions, odd cat states, and GKP states-the protocol provides a statistically bounded pathway for probing quantum features with free electrons.

Abstract: Contemporary classical information processors integrate tens of billions of transistors printed on a chip, yet are fully controlled through comparatively few external wires. This is possible because data flows through hard-coded operations in step with a global clock and is steered by external instructions. Today's solid-state quantum processors are different -- every qubit receives instructions from outside the chip via its own dedicated control line, and error correction adds a further torrent of syndrome data that is transported off-chip. Our quantum processor receives an input stream of classical instructions via a small, constant number of external wires, each attached to a single qubit. Except for those few qubits coupled to external wires, all qubits evolve under a periodically-applied geometrically-local depth-3 circuit, which is fixed at the time the device is fabricated. Assuming a noiseless global clock (so that every operation is applied on schedule, though imperfectly), we establish an accuracy threshold theorem: For physical noise below a universal threshold -- afflicting every gate, measurement, and wire -- a chip with $n$ physical qubits can run any quantum algorithm that uses~$n/{\sf polylog}(n/ε)$ logical qubits, where $ε$ is the error rate per logical gate. Each logical gate takes time~$n/{\sf polylog}(n/ε)$; an alternative design, in which quantum operations remain strictly local and noisy, but on-chip wires broadcast noiseless classical signals instantaneously, reduces the time per logical gate to ${\sf polylog}(n/ε)$.

Abstract: Many studies of quantum protocols require fast sampling of large amounts of measurement data from noisy quantum circuits, such as for training machine-learning decoders for quantum error correction. On 30-qubit random circuits with mid-circuit measurements, it is up to 83x faster and outperforms the near-Clifford simulator Clifft when more than about 25% of the gates are non-Clifford. With repeated sampling of each final state, its sampling rate per trajectory exceeds that of CUDA-Q by nearly five orders of magnitude. Our method also allows us to compare exact amplitude damping with its Pauli-twirled channel on a surface-code memory, revealing that twirling overestimates the detection rate by 21% and raises the logical error rate of a lookup decoder by 8%. This work makes exact, high-throughput simulation practical for general noisy circuits, from data generation for training decoders to studying measurement-based fault-tolerant protocols under realistic noise.

Abstract: By intertwining ideas from sequential and adiabatic quantum computing, we propose a quantum algorithm for the problem class Exact Cover 3, which is a sub-class of 3-satisfiability (3-SAT) problems and NP (nondeterministic polynomial time) complete. Abstract: In standard homodyne detection, an unknown optical signal state is combined with a local oscillator (LO) at a beam splitter (BS), and the appropriately integrated intensities at the two outputs of the BS are subtracted to obtain an approximate quadrature measurement. Accurate quadrature measurements require a high-amplitude LO and high-efficiency detectors. By varying the LO phase, the optical state in the mode matching the LO can be reconstructed. In many practical settings, a high-intensity LO is not desirable, which has led to the exploration of homodyne-like measurement schemes that replace photodiodes with photon-counting detectors and use an LO with a low average photon number. Abstract: The quantum Mpemba effect (QME) describes the counterintuitive phenomenon in which a more strongly perturbed quantum system relaxes toward its steady state faster than a less perturbed one. Here, we investigate the QME in a weakly driven-dissipative atomic array coupled to a one-dimensional chiral waveguide. Starting from the steady state, we introduce a local quench by resetting a single atom to its ground state and examine the subsequent relaxation dynamics. We find that quenching atoms near the array boundaries induces faster initial relaxation through enhanced coupling to superradiant modes and direct photon emission into the waveguide, giving rise to an edge-enhanced QME. Strong directional decay further amplifies this effect by promoting the spatial accumulation of superradiant modes near the preferred boundary, leading to a skin-enhanced QME. At later times, relaxation is increasingly governed by long-lived subradiant modes localized predominantly in the bulk of the array. By coupling two atomic arrays with different interparticle spacings, we further demonstrate an interface-induced QME, where quenching an atom near the interface makes the system relax more slowly at early times but approach its steady state faster at later times, owing to weak participation in both superradiant and subradiant modes. Our findings reveal how the spatial structure of collective decay modes and directional dissipation jointly govern anomalous quantum relaxation, offering potential routes toward accelerated steady-state preparation in waveguide QED systems.

Abstract: In non-Hermitian lattices, combinations of gain, loss and nonreciprocal couplings can generate exponentially growing directional amplification with applications in signal processing and precision measurement. An intriguing question is whether such amplification has a universal topological origin in arbitrary dimensions. Here we establish a dimension-independent framework to uncover the topological origin of directional amplification based on the concept of amoeba, which is the logarithmic image of the singularities of the response generating function. The complement of the amoeba in the logarithmic parameter space consists of connected components, each carrying a distinct and discrete topological label. We show that the asymptotic response is governed by the component extending toward negative infinity. When the origin of the logarithmic parameter space is outside this component and not on its boundary, there must exist some directions in which the response is exponentially amplified. Otherwise, there is no directional amplification in any direction. Our results provide a general topological criterion for classifying the responses in arbitrary-dimensional non-Hermitian lattices.

Abstract: Conservation laws partition the Hilbert space of a quantum system into invariant sectors that are often difficult to identify directly. Repeated measurements on an ancillary probe, however, generate a measurement record that provides information about the occupied sector. Here we develop a framework for identifying conserved sectors using a $(K+1)$-dimensional feature vector of record statistics, comprising the average outcome frequency and pair-outcome correlations up to $K$ measurement cycles apart. For fixed $K$, the feature vector can be evaluated at a cost linear in record length. In quantum non-demolition measurement models, the average outcome frequency suffices for accurate sector identification. For an interacting spin bath with nontrivial dynamics within each sector, incorporating temporal correlations between successive and more widely separated measurements into the record feature vector substantially improves classification fidelity. Our framework provides an efficient approach to conserved-sector identification and demonstrates the importance of temporal correlations in distinguishing the underlying sector dynamics.

Abstract: We construct a family of objective functions $f \colon \{0,1\}^n \to \mathbb{R}$ for which vanilla adiabatic quantum optimization along $H(s) = -(1-s)\sum_{i=1}^n X_i + s\sum_x f(x)\lvert x\rangle\!\langle x\rvert$ finds the global minimizer in $\operatorname{poly}(n)$ time, whereas any classical algorithm requires $\exp(n^{Ω(1)})$ time to do so in the black-box model. Our result is achieved by replacing the linearization technique in an earlier work of Leng et al. (arXiv:2504.14841), which establishes a similar subexponential quantum speedup using a transverse field Ising mixer rather than $-\sum_{i=1}^n X_i$.

</details>

Abstract: Reliable assessment of quantum error correction requires understanding how coherent errors affect logical performance. Although tensor networks naturally capture coherent interference, entanglement growth and large qubit counts make repeated sampling costly. Here we develop a compiled Clifford-Augmented Matrix Product State (CAMPS) framework that reuses Clifford transformations across shots, recycles qubits released by measurements, evolves a compressed residual state, and accelerates sampling through rotation fusion and compilation tailored to the MPS layout. Surface-code benchmarks under fixed-sign coherent noise yield accurate logical error estimates at small bond dimensions, while random-sign simulations up to distance $d=9$ and on the $[[72,12,6]]$ bivariate bicycle code match the Pauli-twirling approximation results. Compared with uncompiled CAMPS, compiled CAMPS converges at smaller bond dimensions and achieves order-of-magnitude sampling speedups at equal bond dimension, opening a scalable route to studying coherent error propagation in larger fault-tolerant circuits.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate odd-even effects on Liouvillian spectra and relaxation dynamics in boundary-driven XY and cluster chains. With the bulk couplings and boundary dissipation rule fixed, the parity of chain length determines whether non-decaying oscillatory modes exist. Consequently, odd- and even-sized chains exhibit qualitatively different dynamical behaviors. We trace this mechanism to the coupling between bulk Majorana paths and the boundary jump operators. An isolated path supports undamped modes, and products of an even number of its Majorana operators span a non?decaying subspace of even operators. This structure yields nonzero purely imaginary eigenvalues of the Liouvillian and persistent contributions to bond correlations or magnetization after preparation in product states. The mechanism extends to cluster interactions of even range under a fixed boundary dissipation rule. The existence and frequencies of the persistent modes are independent of the magnitudes of the positive boundary dissipation rates. Our results unveil how chain parity controls the existence of persistent dynamics.

Abstract: Quantum information can be reliably transmitted at rates below the quantum capacity with an error that vanishes as the number of channel uses grows. Abstract: We present a protocol for implementing a controlled-controlled-Z (CCZ) gate on a neutral-atom platform, employing three individually addressable atoms confined in a linear array of optical tweezers and operating under finite Rydberg-Rydberg interaction (RRI). The protocol combines successive excitation of the Rydberg atoms with analytically determined pulses and uses two Rydberg levels to access opposite-sign interaction shifts. We show two different paths to get the conditional phase in the target atom: a pair of target pulses with opposite phases and a single detuned target pulse followed by a local phase correction. We derive analytical expressions for the time-integrated Rydberg populations to calculate the associated decay errors and also evaluate gate performance through master-equation simulations. Numerical comparisons with a fast Rydberg-antiblockade (RAB)-based CCZ protocol show improved gate performance at larger interatomic separations within the parameter regime considered. Monte Carlo sampling further quantifies the sensitivity to thermally induced position fluctuations. These results identify the tradeoffs between gate duration, Rydberg-state exposure, and interaction sensitivity, providing an analytically specified route to native three-qubit phase gates beyond the strong-blockade regime.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising quantum algorithm for dealing with computationally hard optimization problems. Currently available hardware can implement the algorithm for a significant class of Hamiltonian problems. However, its performance is especially limited in discontinuous quantum phase transitions, where the energy gap shrinks exponentially with system size. In this paper, we introduce an extension of quantum annealing to a quantum neural network and study its performance in a number of prototypical optimization problems. In particular, we observe that already a random quantum annealing network of width W = 2 can exponentially reduce the number of evolution layers or depth of the annealing process in the quantum search problem. By tuning the network parameters the search problem can be solved with exponentially small errors by a quantum perceptron of depth L = 1 and width W = 2. The findings show that width of a quantum annealing network is critical to enhance the success probability of the algorithm by providing a larger and learnable evolution space for the annealing process.

</details>

Abstract: Estimating multivariate traces Tr(A1A2......Ak) is a fundamental task arising across diverse fields. In this paper, We develop a quantum algorithm for this problem tailored to near-term quantum devices. We access matrices by preparing their vectorized states. This access mode yields a tensor-network representation of the multivariate trace, which in turn guides the design of a parallel algorithm. The resulting circuit has depth dominated solely by that of the input oracle and independent of the number of matrices k, at the cost of a qubit count scaling as Θ(k)-a trade-off between circuit depth and circuit size. Moreover, the circuit exhibits a simple modular structure that allows explicit distribution over O(k) devices. We further establish efficient preparation circuits for a broad range of matrix classes, demonstrating the wide applicability of our approach. we present two applications: spectral sum estimation and a quantum implementation of the classical Lanczos method. Both reduce the target problem to sums of multivariate traces, thereby avoiding the construction of matrix polynomial transformations and achieving two levels of parallelism. For certain matrix classes, the resulting algorithms reduce circuit depth by a factor equal to the degree of the approximating polynomial. The shallow circuit depth and distributed implementation make our algorithms particularly suitable for near-term devices.

</details>


### [63] [Gradient Blindness in Adaptive Architecture Search for IQP Born Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24174)
*Sebastian Wölckert,Gerhard Stenzel,Michael Poppel,Claudia Linnhoff-Popien,Jonas Stein*

Motivation: 自适应算法每轮只添加损失梯度最大的候选门，梯度为零的门永不被加入，因此初始种子（seed）隐式限定了算法可达的电路族。此前缺少刻画"哪些候选门对给定种子梯度非零"的理论，种子选择带有盲目性，需要澄清其可及性边界并设计更优种子。

Method: 理论上将候选门在某组电路角度下梯度是否非零，等价刻画为其生成元能否写成电路中已有线性独立生成元的奇数个线性组合。实验上：以单比特门为共同种子，检验偶数比特门是否梯度恒为零（12比特精确梯度验证）；以"单比特门+Chow-Liu树边"为种子，考察其在12、16比特上复现完整电路所需参数比例；在484比特上引入heredity pool限制高阶门搜索空间，并与稠密两体电路比较测试损失。

Result: 单比特门种子永不添加作用于偶数个比特的门，12比特精确梯度实验证实。单比特门加Chow-Liu树边的种子可覆盖所有候选门，其生长电路在12与16比特上以10%–13%的参数达到与完整电路相当的精度。484比特下，heredity pool使高阶门搜索可行，由此种子生长的电路以6%的参数取得比稠密两体电路更低的测试损失，但高阶门未带来额外增益。

Conclusion: 种子的生成元结构决定自适应IQP Born机器的可达电路族；用奇数线性组合判据可预测门可及性。Chow-Liu树边种子实现了近乎全可及且大幅参数压缩，heredity pool支撑大规模高阶搜索；经验上高阶门收益有限，两体结构已足够，为稀疏量子模型的可扩展训练提供指导。

Abstract: Instantaneous quantum polynomial (IQP) Born machines learn a probability distribution from data with a circuit of commuting gates that a classical computer trains at hundreds of qubits. Such algorithms never add a candidate whose gradient vanishes, so the choice of seed decides which circuits they can reach. Here we show that a candidate gate has a nonzero gradient for some angles of the circuit exactly when its generator is the sum of an odd number of linearly independent generators already in the circuit. The common seed of one-qubit gates therefore never adds a gate on an even number of qubits, which exact gradients on 12 qubits confirm. A seed of one-qubit gates plus the edges of the Chow-Liu tree sees every candidate, and its circuits match the full circuits on 12 and 16 qubits with 10 to 13 percent of their parameters. At 484 qubits, a heredity pool keeps the search over higher-order gates feasible, and the circuits grown from this seed reach a lower test loss than the dense two-body circuit with 6 percent of its parameters, although the higher-order gates add no gain.

</details>

Abstract: Two qubit states with the same robustness of imaginarity can differ in the robustness attainable by real filtering on a successful outcome. In arbitrary finite dimensions, maximizing the mean normalized area of the extracted qubits gives the pointwise smallest strongly monotone extension of the qubit measure. We derive computable bounds on this area by determining the maximum mean squared robustness of the output qubits. A projection onto orthogonal real subspaces attains the maximum and, when its outcome is retained, preserves the quantum Fisher information at the midpoint of a mixture of the input and its complex conjugate. Abstract: Quantum imaginarity characterizes the part of a quantum state that cannot be represented by real density matrices in a fixed reference basis. We study an affinity-square measure of imaginarity for pure states and its convex-roof extension to mixed states. The pure state quantity is defined by minimizing the affinity-square measure over the convex set of real states. This optimization admits a closed expression determined by the modulus of the conjugation overlap between a state and its complex conjugate. The measure vanishes precisely when the state is real up to a global phase. We prove that the convex-roof extension is faithful, convex, monotone under real operations, and nonincreasing on average under selective real operations. For arbitrary single-qubit mixed states, we evaluate the convex roof exactly and express it in terms of the imaginary component of the off-diagonal density-matrix element. We also compare pure-state imaginarity with an auxiliary affinity-square coherence functional and give the corresponding phase weights.

</details>


### [66] [QuFrame: Pauli Frame Tracking for Fault-Tolerant Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24328)

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires continuous quantum error correction (QEC) to suppress physical noise. While Clifford-only workloads can defer corrections to software post-processing, universal computation breaks this paradigm: executing non-Clifford gates via magic-state gadgets introduces hard, real-time control-flow dependencies on error-corrected measurements. Consequently, the controller must extract syndromes, decode them, and update the Pauli frame in real time, closing the entire QEC loop within a strict $1\,μ\text{s}$ cycle. However, prior proposals on Pauli frame tracking remain largely theoretical, leaving the microarchitectural bottlenecks of tracking, resolving, and synchronizing frame updates across distributed control pipelines unaddressed.

By introducing QuFrameISA, we cleanly decouple instruction execution from continuous Pauli frame management. Execution is distributed across per-qubit Instruction Resolution Engines (IREs) that resolve instructions against the frame via a single table lookup, while a Pauli Frame Management Unit enables independent, lock-free concurrent commits. To strictly guarantee the $1\,μ\text{s}$ deadline, QuFrame pairs an asynchronous primary decoder with an on-chip fallback decoder that resolves in $144\,\text{ns}$, natively reconciling divergent updates in hardware without degrading logical error rates.

</details>

Result: 无噪声模型下，1 Hz采样保持校正速率使相位基QBER低于2%，保留理想渐近密钥产率的93%；经18次累积过境，校正后有限密钥长度达理想值的95%，而仅依据星历重标定奇偶性、不做光学补偿仅约8%。

Conclusion: Doppler致相位漂移可借零Doppler标定在本地解耦并校正，星历辅助前馈+见证场测相的光学补偿方案无需站间实时相位交换即可显著恢复相位基性能，为星地时-bin纠缠QKD提供可行架构；结论基于无噪声模型，尚需考虑实际噪声与工程误差。

Abstract: Time-bin entanglement is promising for satellite Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) due to its compatibility with telecom fiber networks, but its phase-sensitive measurements are vulnerable to Doppler effects induced by satellite motion. We present a spectral-temporal framework for time-bin-entangled photon pairs generated onboard a satellite via Spontaneous Parametric Down Conversion (SPDC) and distributed to two Optical Ground Stations (OGSs) over independent downlinks with distinct Doppler shifts. Each OGS is equipped with an Unbalanced Mach-Zehnder Interferometer (UMZI) used to perform Bennett-Brassard-Mermin 1992 (BBM92) entangled-photon QKD. In a simulated Low Earth Orbit (LEO) pass with matched 400 ps analyzer delays, the received time-bin separation changes by at most 8.8 fs per link, while the combined carrier phase shifts by about 38 rad, which primarily degrades phase-basis correlations. After zero-Doppler calibration, this phase separates into independent signal-and idler-link contributions, allowing each station to correct its link Doppler phase shift locally, without real-time phase exchange between the stations. We propose an ephemeris-assisted feed-forward architecture, in which an auxiliary interferometer analyzes a co-propagating classical witness field to reconstruct the local Doppler-induced phase, which a phase modulator then compensates on the quantum channel. In a noise-free model, a 1 Hz sample-and-hold correction rate keeps the phase-basis Quantum Bit Error Rate (QBER) below 2% and retains 93% of the ideal asymptotic key yield. After 18 accumulated passes, the corrected finite secret key reaches 95% of its ideal length, whereas relabeling the parity from the ephemeris without optical correction reaches only about 8%.

Abstract: Continuous-variable (CV) entanglement offers deterministic generation and room-temperature coherent detection for quantum networks, but distributing it over deployed fibre has been hindered by the need to stabilise optical phase, polarisation, and timing at remote receivers. Here we demonstrate, to our knowledge, the first distribution of CV entanglement over deployed metropolitan fibre using independent coherent receivers. The two modes of a two-mode squeezed vacuum state are delivered to remote nodes through 23 km ($\approx 7$ dB loss) and 450 m ($\approx 1$ dB loss) deployed fibre links. Each node uses its own free-running laser and polarisation-diverse coherent receiver. Co-propagating pilot tones enable digital recovery of timing, phase and polarisation, while inter-mode correlations fix the quadrature alignment. We certify entanglement with a Simon-Duan value of $1.915\pm 0.013$, below the separability bound of 2 and a logarithmic negativity of $0.067\pm0.011$. By replacing optical stabilisation with digital reconstruction, our approach offers a practical route towards scalable CV quantum networks.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在所测实现中，直接Pauli演化对复杂分子VHA更有利；GPU对更宽的spin与硬件高效电路加速明显。MPS在纠缠受限、可精确压缩时有效。经校正的CPU FP32可满足测试容差但成本高于FP64。匹配的NumPy Pauli更新使两层完整LiH加速3.45倍；FP64下GPU相较18线程Aer在28量子比特、四层TFIM上约加速30倍。

Conclusion: 工作负载规模、启动开销、worker内存及任务级剖面都会改变最优资源分配；作者给出在精度、内存与核心约束下对表示、精度与worker分配进行联合选择的流程，并以完整能量评估成本作为对比指标。

Abstract: Classical circuit simulation supports quantum software development and verification and provides a baseline for assessing quantum advantage. We compare gate-based statevector simulation, direct Pauli evolution and matrix product states for molecular and spin Variational Hamiltonian Ansatze and hardware-efficient ansatze on CPU/GPU systems, varying width, depth, precision and resource allocation. In the tested implementations, direct Pauli evolution benefits complex molecular VHA, whereas GPUs accelerate wider spin and hardware efficient circuits. Matrix product state is useful when limited entanglement permits accurate compression; corrected CPU FP32 can meet the tested tolerance while costing more than FP64. Workload size, startup, worker memory and task-specific profiling can change the preferred allocation. Matched NumPy Pauli updates accelerate two-layer full LiH by 3.45-fold; the GPU gives approximately 30-fold acceleration over 18-thread Aer for 28-qubit, four-layer TFIM, both in FP64. We provide a procedure for choosing representation, precision and worker allocation jointly, comparing the cost of complete energy evaluations under accuracy, memory and core constraints.

</details>

*F. Güngör,J. Negro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于等价变换与李点对称性重新研究量子力学中的含时谐振子(TDHO)，给出含七参数的最一般点变换实现TDHO与常谐振子的相互映射，并不依赖Lewis–Riesenfeld不变量，通过李代数方法导出TDHO的归一化波函数。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上TDHO的求解高度依赖Lewis–Riesenfeld(LR)不变量方法，其与体系李对称结构的关系尚不清晰；作者希望以等价变换和李点对称性为统一框架重新审视TDHO，厘清LR不变量在对称性结构中的地位。

Method: 采用等价变换与李点对称性分析：构造含七个参数的最一般点变换，在TDHO与常谐振子(HO)之间双向映射；作为副产品重构HO的Schrödinger传播子(Mehler核)；在SL(2,ℝ)子群与规范群组合下构造不变解，从而不借助LR不变量导出归一化波函数；并证明在SL(2,ℝ)不变解族上，LR算子构成相容的二阶微分约束。

Result: 得到TDHO与HO之间七参数最一般点变换；重构出时无关谐振子的Mehler核；无需LR不变量即通过李代数方法给出TDHO归一化波函数的替代推导；表明LR算子在相关SL(2,ℝ)不变解族上等价于相容的二阶微分约束。

Conclusion: 李对称与等价变换方法可作为TDHO的系统性求解框架，LR不变量可被理解为该对称结构下相容的二阶微分约束，从而澄清其与李对称结构的联系。

Abstract: We revisit the time-dependent harmonic oscillator (TDHO) in quantum mechanics based on the notion of equivalence transformations and Lie point symmetries. Within this framework, we give the most general point transformations depending on seven parameters which map from the TDHO to the usual harmonic oscillator (HO) and the other way around. As a byproduct we reconstruct the Schrödinger propagator (Mehler's kernel) for the time-independent harmonic oscillator. We demonstrate, without recourse to the Lewis--Riesenwert (LR) invariant, how Lie algebraic methods enable alternative derivation of the normalized wave functions of the TDHO by constructing solutions invariant under some specific subgroup of $\SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ combined with the gauge group. Abstract: We introduce virtual autopurification (VAP), a protocol for virtual purification that operates entirely within a single translationally invariant system. VAP avoids the multiple-copy overhead by constructing virtual copies from spatially separated subregions and performing entangled measurements only between them. Assuming clustering of correlations, we show that VAP reproduces the expectation values of the standard virtual purification protocol, with discrepancies that decay exponentially in both the buffer length and the inter-subregion distance. The measurements revealed an external photon detection efficiency (PDE) of 42 % to 55 %, and a dark count rate (DCR) of 2.6 MHz to 17 MHz, for an amplitude threshold ranging from 2.5 to 4.5 times the rms noise of the amplifier. Abstract: Entangled states that are positive under partial transposition (PPT) are the main obstruction to a simple description of entanglement. We study three basic questions about them within a class of multiqubit states, the mixtures, with real but possibly negative weights, of products of equatorial spin coherent states of several groups of qubits: which PPT entangled states belong to the class, how robust their entanglement is to white noise, and how small their rank can be. Every such state is PPT across every bipartition, and it is fully separable exactly when an associated multi-level Toeplitz matrix is the moment matrix of a positive measure on the torus. We answer the three questions exactly in the following cases. For three qubits, every rank-four PPT entangled state with nonzero Lorentz invariant belongs to the class after invertible local operators, which proves a working hypothesis of Garberg, Irgens and Myrheim and yields a complete set of invariants for this seven-dimensional family; among the states with vanishing invariant, exactly a one-parameter family belongs to it. For every three-qubit state built from an unextendible product basis, the white-noise threshold for full separability is $λ^*=1-2/(1+\sqrt{1+s})$ with a local-unitary invariant $s\in(0,1]$, and $λ^*=3-2\sqrt2$ for the Shifts basis; a witness in closed form detects every entangled state of this family with eight local Pauli settings. For two groups of qubits we construct extremal states of minimal rank, and for three groups we determine the minimal rank of entanglement within the class in several configurations.

Abstract: The protocol introduced by C. H. Bennett, G. Brassard and N. D. Mermin in 1992 (BBM92) is one of the most important and widely adopted quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols. As with any other QKD protocol, its practical security depends on satisfying the assumptions underlying its theoretical security proofs. We characterise the passive polarisation analysers common to both architectures and present experimental results obtained with the time-multiplexed receiver, employing a continuous-wave (CW) pumped spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) source of polarisation-entangled photon pairs and threshold detectors. Abstract: Simulating the real-time dynamics of correlated electron systems requires a nonperturbative treatment of local correlations over long times, a challenge for classical methods. We present the time-dependent Gutzwiller quantum-classical embedding framework, t-GQCE, for generic multi-orbital lattice models with time-dependent Hamiltonians. Within the time-dependent ghost Gutzwiller approximation, the lattice dynamics is mapped onto a noninteracting quasiparticle lattice, propagated classically, coupled to finite interacting embedding models propagated on quantum processors. The two parts exchange only embedding Hamiltonian parameters and single-particle density matrices, and the framework is agnostic to the quantum dynamics solver. We demonstrate it with adaptive variational quantum dynamics simulations of interaction quenches in the Hubbard model on the Bethe lattice. Without ghost orbitals, the embedding problem reduces to a single qubit, simulated on IBM quantum hardware with two qubits, including an ancilla. With more ghost orbitals, t-GQCE suppresses the artificial undamped oscillations of the conventional Gutzwiller approximation and approaches nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory results for weak and strong quenches. Statevector simulations with 2, 4 and 6 ghost orbitals per spin, or 8, 12 and 16 qubits, give adaptive circuits that follow the embedding states with infidelities below $5\times10^{-4}$. Under trapped-ion noise models, circuit compression, measurements in rotated orbital bases with post-selection, and noise mitigation reduce the measurement errors severalfold. At one third of the error rates of a hardware-benchmarked noise model, they reach an accuracy of $0.01$ for the single-particle density matrix and the double occupancy with $10^5$ shots per time step, and the Quantinuum H2-Emulator gives similar accuracy.

</details>

Result: 证明：对任意固定有限 n≥2，当输入取遍高于阈值 F* 的全族态时，任何从不降低最高输入保真度的可分成功映射，在成功时必须精确保留该最高保真度；该结论对任意高的输入保真度均成立，且不要求成功概率有正下界。

Conclusion: 在可分操作下不存在非平凡的通用保真度排序纯化，将既有 no-go 结果从受限 LOCC 类推广到可分操作类，涵盖任意高输入保真度且无需成功概率下界假设，对量子中继等分布式量子信息处理中的纠缠管理策略具有限制性意义。

Abstract: Entanglement purification is a fundamental tool for obtaining high-fidelity entangled states used in various quantum technologies. It aims to increase the fidelity of noisy shared entangled states with a fixed maximally entangled state through LOCC. In distributed quantum systems, such as networks employing quantum repeaters, limited memory, probabilistic entanglement generation, and unequal storage times can leave a finite collection of nonidentical pairs. Their states may remain incompletely characterized even when calibrated preparation and storage records establish their fidelity ordering. Recent work has investigated whether fidelity ordering permits nontrivial universal purification. A nontrivial universal $n$-to-1 protocol must, conditioned on success, never lower the highest input fidelity and strictly increase it for at least one allowed joint input. Although no-go results have been established for a restricted class of LOCC protocols, the corresponding question for general LOCC has remained unresolved. Here, we study ordered universal purification under separable operations. Specifically, for every fixed finite $n\ge2$, we prove that when independently prepared two-qubit pairs range over the full family of states above a fixed fidelity threshold $0\le F_*<1$, any separable success map that never lowers the highest input fidelity on the allowed ordered inputs must preserve that fidelity exactly whenever it succeeds. This excludes nontrivial universal LOCC purification even for arbitrarily high input fidelities, without imposing a positive lower bound on success probability. ...

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对Lüders仪器定义总变差距离T、Jensen-Shannon散度J与平方Hellinger距离三种顺序分数，刻画其性质（对易时为零、二元锐观测下T=‖[A,B]‖/4）；建立弱形式（如Maassen-Uffink界q>0蕴含T≥1−2^{−q}）与强形式，将Bell/相关性不等式的违反界转化为各Party顺序分数的双线性界；对二元锐观测给出Bell算子平方的对称化(Jordan)部分减局域对易子张量积的分解，并使用Hilbert-Schmidt型Grothendieck不等式；对Bell原不等式与KCBS五边形给出具体的顺序效应表达。

Result: 顺序分数在对易时恰为零；某些基对比MUB在平方Hellinger分数上更大、奇维下J更大，尽管最小熵和更小；q>0给出T和J的正下界。相关性Bell不等式违反在单方两设置时被双方顺序分数双线性界住；在Clifford实现下可由所证的Hilbert-Schmidt型Grothendieck不等式推出（一方≤3设置时）。三二元锐测量下，最大纠缠态违反Bell原不等式等于最大混合态三测量记录中的顺序效应减去四倍交替概率；qutrit上秩一KCBS的违反不超过两条相交对角线的顺序分数之积，且在最大违反处取等号。

Conclusion: 顺序记录为量化非对易性提供了一个统一且可操作的框架：它既复现并强化熵不确定性界，又把语境性、导引与Bell违反表述为顺序分数（及其乘积）的界，并给出Bell原不等式与KCBS五边形的精确等式关系及一个Grothendieck型不等式的部分证明。

Abstract: Two quantum measurements applied to the same input in opposite orders give two joint distributions of the same outcomes. For Lüders instruments we measure how far apart these order records can be, over all inputs, by their total-variation distance $\mathcal{T}$, their Jensen-Shannon divergence $\mathcal{J}$ (in bits), and their squared Hellinger distance. These order scores vanish exactly when the measurements commute, and $\mathcal{T}=\|[A,B]\|/4$ for two binary sharp observables. Some pairs of bases exceed mutually unbiased bases in squared Hellinger score, and in odd dimensions in $\mathcal{J}$, although their minimal entropy sum is smaller. For projective measurements, a Maassen-Uffink bound $q>0$ implies $\mathcal{T}\ge1-2^{-q}$ and a positive lower bound on $\mathcal{J}$. Measuring all observables of a scenario in sequence defines a joint distribution, so violations of noncontextuality, steering, and Bell inequalities are bounded by distances between order records. For correlation Bell inequalities with binary sharp observables, the squared Bell operator is a symmetrized (Jordan) part minus tensor products of local commutators. If one party has two settings, the violation is bounded bilinearly in the two parties' order scores; we conjecture this bound in general. On Clifford realizations it would follow from a Hilbert-Schmidt variant of Grothendieck's inequality, which we prove when one party has at most three settings. For three binary sharp measurements, the violation of Bell's original inequality by a maximally entangled state equals an order effect in three-measurement records of the maximally mixed state minus four times an alternation probability. For the rank-one Klyachko-Can-Binicioğlu-Shumovsky pentagon on a qutrit, the violation is at most the largest product of the order scores of two crossing diagonals, with equality at the maximal violation.

Method: 在MZ干涉仪中嵌入量子存储：利用离共振阶梯型原子相互作用，通过驱动控制场把单光子激发在存储-读取过程中映射为分布于不同光学模式的一对光子；干涉仪结构使该过程在光子数基下确定性地实现 $\sum_n a_n|n\rangle \to \sum_n a_n|n\rangle|n\rangle$ 的有效变换。

Abstract: Strong photon-photon interactions are central to quantum optical technologies yet remain challenging to realise at the single-photon level. Here, we show how embedding a quantum memory within a Mach-Zehnder interferometer enables a coherent transformation that converts single-mode photon states into number-correlated entangled states. Using an off-resonant ladder-type atomic interaction, storage and retrieval within a quantum memory maps a single-photon excitation onto a pair of photons across distinct optical modes via a driving control field. Embedding this within an interferometer deterministically produces an effective transformation in the photon number basis: $\sum_n a_n |n\rangle \to \sum_n a_n |n\rangle |n\rangle$ without relying on conventional optical nonlinearities. Measurement of one mode enables photonic non-demolition detection, state preparation, and frequency conversion as applications of the same underlying transformation. These results establish quantum memories embedded within an interferometric architecture as a platform for measurement-induced photon-photon interactions and the generation of Gaussian and non-Gaussian entangled states.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个在每一步都能与精确解对照的数字化量子模拟基准，而瞬子介导的隧穿（能级分裂）提供了经典可算的严格答案；同时希望利用经典可得的一元单激发动力学，把格点模型的量子模拟拆成可验证的部件。

Method: 采用一元（单激发）编码将N个格点映射到N个量子比特；构造二阶Trotter电路，每条键上使用相邻等角度的XX/YY对；证明任意切割处Schmidt秩在各门边界至多为4、每对门之后至多为2，且与N无关；用矩阵乘积态模拟该门序列并与精确分裂对比；在超导处理器上以较粗且单独界定的步长在N=8执行完整转移测量，并以测量得到的单激发布居做归一化。

Result: MPS模拟在N≤48范围内复现精确分裂；按键拆分的公式下可容许Trotter步长约按N^-2收缩，一个隧穿周期约耗N^3.3个双比特门；动力学/势能拆分可在N=128内保持步长约0.065，说明该标度源自逐键拆分动能项。N=8实验中分裂恢复到几个百分点，并复现其对势垒高度的依赖（此分辨率下由格点而非瞬子决定）。9次重复中，相隔数分钟的运行与散粒噪声一致，相隔数小时至数周相差达6.1%（固定量子比特亦然）；以session为采样单位时各处理器阻尼不同，分裂差异未被确立。按单激发布居归一化使可用深度扩展约4倍，但未改善分裂精度。高斯起始态（无需对角化）与特征向量起始态的分裂相差不超过1.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 该瞬子-格点模型构成逐步骤可检验的数字化量子模拟基准：一元编码与键式Trotter方案给出与N无关的低纠缠结构和可控误差标度，MPS可精确复现经典结果，硬件在N=8忠实复现分裂及其势垒依赖，但长期硬件漂移（数周量级可达6.1%）是当前精度瓶颈，归一化与简化起始态可显著降低资源而不损精度。

Abstract: We use instanton-mediated tunnelling in the quartic double well as a benchmark for digital quantum simulation that is checkable against an exact answer at every step. The instanton enters through the classical analysis; the hardware tests the dynamics of the lattice model. A unary (single-excitation) encoding maps N lattice sites to N qubits, with the single-excitation dynamics as a reference that remains classically cheap at any N. For a second-order Trotter circuit with adjacent, equal-angle XX/YY pairs on each bond, we prove that the Schmidt rank across any cut is at most four at every gate boundary and at most two after each pair, independently of N. Matrix-product-state simulations of this gate list reproduce the exact splitting up to N = 48. For this bond-wise formula the admissible Trotter step shrinks roughly as N^-2, so one tunnelling period costs about N^3.3 two-qubit gates; a kinetic/potential splitting keeps the step near 0.065 up to N=128, so this scaling belongs to splitting the kinetic term bond by bond. At a coarser, separately bounded step we measure the full transfer at N=8 on two superconducting processors, recovering the lattice splitting to a few percent and its dependence on the barrier height, which at this resolution is set by the lattice rather than by the instanton. Over 9 repetitions, runs taken minutes apart agree to shot noise, while runs hours to weeks apart differ by up to 6.1%, also on a fixed set of qubits; with sessions as the sampling unit, the processors differ in the damping, while a difference in the splitting is not established. Normalising by the measured single-excitation population extends the usable depth about 4-fold but does not improve the splitting. A Gaussian start state, which needs no diagonalisation, reproduces the splitting of the eigenvector-based start to within 1.6 percentage points.

</details>

Motivation: OTOC与信息扰乱、量子混沌密切相关，高阶OTOC估计被视为量子优势的候选任务，但其经典难度尚不清楚；即便一阶OTOC已有大量证据表明经典可处理，此前仍缺乏带严格保证的高效经典算法。

Method: 提出window propagation（窗口传播）方法：在一维Haar随机brickwork电路中沿光锥局部化地传播窗口，从而在多项式时间内近似计算一阶OTOC；并开展数值实验，将模拟结果与精确计算对比。

Abstract: Out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) are quantities of broad interest in many-body physics due to their connection with information scrambling and quantum chaos. Recently, estimating higher-order OTOCs in random quantum circuits was proposed as a promising avenue for quantum advantage, yet the classical hardness of this task remains poorly understood. Even for first-order OTOCs, where growing evidence suggests classical tractability, no efficient classical algorithm with rigorous guarantees has been established to date. In this work, we introduce "window propagation," the first provably polynomial-time classical algorithm for approximating first-order OTOCs in 1D Haar-random brickwork circuits, at arbitrary depths and with inverse-polynomial mean-squared error. We also conduct numerical experiments comparing our simulation results to exact computation on up to $512$ qubits for Clifford circuits with $768$ $T$-gates and up to $22$ qubits for Haar-random circuits. We emphasize that our results do not directly apply to second-order OTOCs in 2D, which is the subject of recent quantum advantage proposals. Nevertheless, our work provides a rigorous step towards understanding the classical complexity of OTOC estimation and represents a family of non-trivial quantum circuits for which a provable, polynomial time algorithm is now known, establishing foundations for understanding these widely used diagnostics of quantum chaos.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 反应深度是可形式化且具实践价值的优化目标，但需区分线路与其运行层面的定义及magic depth的影响。在受限但适配LDPC码的门集下，常数反应深度的布尔逻辑与高效算术原语是可行的，为降低量子程序运行时间提供了理论与构造基础。

Abstract: Reducing runtimes of quantum applications is critical for practical viability. On quantum computers, the runtime can depend both on the speed of quantum operations and on how often the program must wait for the classical coprocessor to determine adaptive operations. Some recent approaches to reducing this overhead have focused on minimizing the "reaction depth" of a computation, informally defined as the number of times the classical coprocessor must be consulted while running a quantum program.
  In this work, we formalize the notion of reaction depth. We find several subtleties in this theory. For example, we distinguish the reaction depth of a circuit from that of a circuit run, and show that the magic depth of a unitary can be higher than the reaction depth once compiled into Clifford computation and magic state injection.

We use a natural gateset for fast operations with Low-Density Parity Check (LDPC) codes, which consists of homogeneous $Z$- and $X$-type Pauli Product Measurements (PPMs), alongside a supply of Toffoli states. We prove that, using this gateset, any Boolean arithmetic circuit can be implemented on a quantum computer with constant quantum depth and constant reaction depth, at the cost of exponential space and leaving some qubits "dirty". Using the same gateset we find two efficient AutoAND circuits that require only a Toffoli state and homogeneous PPMs. We then give efficient implementations of the quantum ripple-carry adder and quantum schoolbook multiplier, which require a low number of heterogeneous PPMs for uncomputation. We find implementations competitive with the state-of-the-art despite the restricted gateset.

</details>
