Abstract: Magnetic sensing with cavity--magnon systems typically relies on the coherent response, while field-dependent fluctuations are treated as measurement noise. Here, we show that the variance of a measured microwave quadrature provides a distinct source of magnetic-field information. For repeated phase-sensitive measurements of a calibrated quadrature, we derive the Gaussian likelihood and show that its Fisher information separates exactly into two terms. The coherent-displacement term depends on the field derivative of the quadrature mean, and the thermal-fluctuation term depends on the field derivative of its variance. Both terms are accessible from the same measurement record, but their optimal operating conditions differ. For the amplitude quadrature, the dimensionless coherent response factor reaches its global maximum at cooperativity $C=2$, corresponding to a coupling strength $g=\sqrt{2κ_aκ_m}$, and at linewidth-normalized detunings $δ_a=δ_m=\pm 1$. In the near-resonance approximation, the reduced variance-information term is globally maximized at cavity--magnon frequency matching, $Δ_a=Δ_m$, and approaches a finite limit at large cooperativity. We map the relative contributions of the two terms across drive frequencies, drive strengths, and temperatures. Strong coherent driving favors displacement-based sensing, whereas at finite temperature the variance contribution can dominate under weak driving and remain finite even when the coherent-displacement contribution vanishes. These results establish a measurement-specific framework for cavity--magnon magnetometry and identify operating regimes for exploiting the quadrature mean, its variance, or both.

</details>

Abstract: How much quantum memory is needed to faithfully retain the entanglement spectrum of a bipartite pure state after separating out Bell pairs through local unitaries? We derive the exact tradeoff between Bell-pair yield, residual memory, and reconstruction fidelity under local unitaries, defining an operational measure of entanglement-spectrum complexity. Smoothed entanglement spread bounds this memory cost from below, while diverging nonlocal magic forces unbounded memory at any fixed positive fidelity. Chaotic circuit dynamics exhibit a transient memory peak whose height increases with system size, followed by declining cost despite continued entropy growth. A large typical Haar-random state requires only an accuracy-dependent memory, whereas stationary fermionic Gaussian states retain a growing cost with size. In the circuits studied, the multiscale spectral structure associated with entanglement catalysis also has a practical consequence for matrix product state compression, yielding a multiplicative overhead not captured by the conventional entropy-based bond dimension estimate.

Abstract: A squeezed laser undergoes a lasing transition into a squeezed cavity mode, where stimulated emission generates a bright coherent field with a laser-like coherence time while maintaining one field quadrature below the vacuum noise level. Here we propose an optical implementation that needs neither a nonlinear crystal nor injected squeezed light, based on engineered interactions between ensembles of rubidium atoms and a squeezed cavity mode via cavity-assisted Raman transitions. A first ensemble cools the cavity into a squeezed vacuum, a mechanism we term squeezed cooling, yielding up to 9 dB in the intracavity field, surpassing the 3 dB limit of optical parametric oscillators. Tuned for output instead, it reaches up to 18 dB of output squeezing. Incoherently pumping a second ensemble drives a lasing transition into the squeezed mode, emitting a bright beam with long coherence time and noise below vacuum spanning a megahertz-wide band, away from the sharp phase-diffusion peak of excess noise at the carrier frequency, and robust to relaxation oscillations. We characterize lasing thresholds, superradiant emission, and linewidth narrowing, and identify the experimentally accessible regime where the system exhibits the defining features of a squeezed laser.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FALQON无需经典参数优化即可制备基态，但电路深、受贫瘠高原与对称性保护影响会停滞在非基态；在闭系中直接加入非幺正ITE层不可实现，实际硬件上近似又受限，故需开放系统框架。

Method: 把ITE刻画为由环境诱导的量子操作，并与FALQON反馈律结合，构成CITE-FALQON；在受挫Ising链(ANNNI)与MaxCut问题上进行数值模拟，比较不同初态下的收敛性与层数。

Conclusion: 开放系统下的ITE与反馈型量子优化结合可缓解停滞、降低电路深度需求，但其增益具有问题与参数区间的依赖性。

Abstract: The Feedback-based Algorithm for Quantum Optimization (FALQON) prepares ground states without classical parameter optimization, but requires deep circuits and can stagnate away from the ground state as a consequence of barren plateaus and symmetry protection. In order to avoid stagnation, imaginary time evolution (ITE) layers can be incorporated into FALQON layers, enhancing FALQON convergence. However, applying a non-unitary ITE layer in a closed quantum system is not possible, and approximations must be made to use ITE on real hardware; using an open quantum system formulation to incorporate ITE into FALQON represents a better approach. We describe ITE as a quantum operation induced by an environment, and combine it with the FALQON feedback law. In simulations of a frustrated Ising chain (ANNNI) and of MaxCut, the resulting algorithm prepares the ground state starting from any initial state, where FALQON can fail, and needs fewer layers on average when the initial state is fixed for the two investigated problems. The CITE-FALQON advantage strongly depends on the system phase for the ANNNI chain, and on the graph weights for the MaxCut problem.

</details>


### [89] [Soft Mixture of Quantum Experts for Hybrid Quantum-Classical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24798)
*Valeria Repetto,Romina D'Aurizio,Piero Farinelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Soft-MoQE：用输入相关的固定量子特征映射选择与组合（软混合量子专家）替代变分量子训练，在不优化任何量子参数的情况下达到可训练量子神经网络的性能，量子训练成本低几个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于可训练参数化量子线路的QML架构受限于硬件资源、噪声以及贫瘠高原等严重优化困难，亟需可训练性友好的替代设计。

Method: 构建异构的非可训练量子线路池，每条线路定义一种不同的量子嵌入；用经典门控网络为每个数据点学习各嵌入的加权组合，从而以软混合专家方式实现输入相关的特征路由。

Result: 在三个PCA降维任务（MNIST奇偶、MNIST十类、FashionMNIST）上，Soft-MoQE优于最佳单专家、全体专家均匀平均与线性基线，性能与可训练量子神经网络相当且依任务而定；类条件专家使用分析显示每条输入仅组合少数专家，且选择与类别相关。

Conclusion: 在固定量子线路之间进行可学习路由是一种兼顾可训练性的混合QML设计原则。

Abstract: Quantum Machine Learning (QML) has attracted increasing interest as a possible route to construct feature representations that are difficult to reproduce with classical models. However, near-term QML architectures based on trainable parametrized quantum circuits remain affected by limited hardware resources, noise, and severe optimization difficulties such as barren plateaus. In this work we propose Soft-MoQE, a Soft Mixture of Quantum Experts architecture that replaces variational quantum training with the input-dependent selection and combination of fixed quantum feature maps. The model is composed of a heterogeneous pool of non-trainable quantum circuits, each defining a distinct embedding, and a classical gating network that learns how to weight these embeddings for each data point. We benchmark Soft-MoQE on three PCA-reduced tasks: MNIST parity, MNIST 10-class, and FashionMNIST. Soft-MoQE outperforms the single best expert, the uniform average of all experts and a linear baseline, achieving task-dependent performance comparable to that of a trainable quantum neural network, without optimizing any quantum parameter and with a quantum training cost that is lower by orders of magnitude. The analysis of the class- conditioned expert usage shows that the learned routing combines a few experts for each input, with a selection that depends on the class. These results support the learned routing among fixed quantum circuits as a trainability-aware design principle for hybrid QML.

</details>


### [90] [The limits of magic-state cultivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24852)
*Llorenç Balada Gaggioli,Jakub Mareček*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了针对含非Clifford门与后选择电路（如魔态培养）的精确容错理论，指出存在一个作用于非Clifford门上的稳定子码决定故障是否有害，并据此给出T-count下界、精确检验，以及对现有S门代理与相邻检查方案的量化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pauli代数仅能刻画Clifford门下的容错，在非Clifford门处失效，导致magic-state cultivation等用物理T门检验编码T态的电路缺乏"哪些故障有害"的精确判据，现有多数设计只能用S门作代理，其可靠性未知。

Method: 构造一个作用于非Clifford门上的稳定子码（类比于Pauli代数在Clifford情形的角色），用以判定任一含非Clifford门及后选择的魔态制备电路中的有害故障；对理想编码态的单次检查，将该判据退化为码上的Pauli代数；并以S门代理检查（color/surface code）为案例进行故障距离分析，同时设计保持完整距离的最近邻检查。

Result: 证明了该单一稳定子码可精确判定有害故障；把cultivation与distillation联系起来并给出T-count下界与精确检验；证明S门代理只会高估检查的故障距离；现有color/surface code检查最多只能容忍其码所允许故障数的一半；构造的最近邻检查保持完整距离，并在码距9以内获得认证。

Conclusion: 非Clifford电位的容错可由一个非Clifford门上的稳定子码精确刻画，从而为培育类电路提供可判定的故障理论与下界；现有基于S门代理的检查存在本质性距离损失，而所提出的最近邻检查可在可达码距上恢复完整故障距离。

Abstract: Fault tolerance is understood through Pauli algebra, which fails at non-Clifford gates. Circuits such as magic-state cultivation, which checks an encoded T state with physical T gates, have therefore lacked an exact theory of which faults are harmful. We develop such a theory and show that, for any circuit that prepares a magic state with non-Clifford gates and postselection, one stabilizer code on the non-Clifford gates decides which faults are harmful. This ties cultivation to distillation, bounds its T-count from below and gives an exact test. For a single check on an ideally encoded state, the fault distance reduces to Pauli algebra on the code. As a use case, we analyze the S gates that have stood in for T gates in the design of cultivation. We show that this proxy can only overstate the fault distance of such a check, and that the checks in use, on color and surface codes, tolerate at most half as many faults as their code allows. The theory also yields nearest-neighbor checks that keep the full distance, certified up to code distance nine.

</details>


### [91] [Hurst Exponent Estimation with Quantum Wavelets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24882)
*Brani Vidakovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在每次测量都探测同一条随机路径（单条幅度编码路径的重复制备）时，分数布朗运动 Hurst 指数 $H$ 的估计精度问题；通过改进 $\epsilon$-抽取量子非抽取小波变换、推导估计量抽样分布并比较等查询预算下的读取方案，给出根-$N$ 中心极限定理及多种读取方式的精度对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答"当所有测量都 interrogate 同一实现（single realization）时，$H$ 能多精确地被估计"这一问题。传统 $\epsilon$-抽取量子非抽取小波变换存在循环滤波的端点跳变问题，且缺乏估计量的抽样分布理论与不同量子读取方案在相同查询预算下的公平比较。

Method: 沿三个方向扩展 $\epsilon$-抽取量子非抽取小波变换：(1) 用单量子比特镜像延拓消除循环滤波的端点跳变，使跨折叠处的系数与内部系数同阶（对任意紧支撑滤波器与循环移位成立）；变换由其滤波器组的仿酉格编译，层能量份额等于一次多控反射的成功概率（既是 Hadamard 检验的受控酉，又是幅度估计的 oracle）；(2) 对至少两个消失矩的滤波器，证明对数能量对比的根-$N$ 中心极限定理（独立于极限路径范数），在学生化估计量与各层成功计数发散时为渐近标准正态；稀疏计数下给出多项式–对数正态似然模型并仿真检验覆盖率；(3) 在等查询预算下比较 Hadamard 层检验与直接测量前导量子比特的精度。

Result: 对长度 512 的路径，镜像使 $H\ge0.5$ 时 RMSE 降低 $3$–$10$ 倍；在精确份额下非抽取 db2 估计量的 RMSE 高于根 Cramér–Rao 界 $6$–$14\%$。Hadamard 层检验在各预算下均不如直接测量前导量子比特。$H=0.9$、$2^{20}$ 查询时，极大似然幅度估计达 RMSE $0.0347$，直接测量为 $0.0451$，精确份额下为 $0.0295$。

Conclusion: 镜像延拓有效消除了端点伪影并显著提升估计精度；非抽取 db2 估计量接近但未达到根 Cramér–Rao 界；在等预算比较中，幅度估计（极大似然）优于直接测量，而 Hadamard 层检验精度最差。所建立的根-$N$ 中心极限定理与多项式–对数正态似然为确定性查询预算与稀疏计数两种情形提供了理论保证。

Abstract: How accurately can the Hurst exponent $H$ of fractional Brownian motion be estimated from repeated preparations of a single amplitude-encoded path, when every measurement interrogates the same realization? We extend the $ε$-decimated quantum nondecimated wavelet transform in three directions: we remove the endpoint jump of circular filtering, derive the sampling distribution of the estimator, and compare readouts at equal query budgets. A one-qubit mirror extension removes the jump and keeps every coefficient that straddles a fold of the same order as an interior coefficient, for any compactly supported filter and circular shift. The transform for any such filter is compiled from the paraunitary lattice of its filter bank, and the energy share of a level is the success probability of one multiply-controlled reflection, which is both the controlled unitary of a Hadamard test and the oracle of amplitude estimation. For filters with at least two vanishing moments we prove a root-$N$ central limit theorem for the log-energy contrasts, independent of the limiting path norm, so the studentized estimator is asymptotically standard normal for deterministic query budgets under which the success count at every level diverges. For sparse counts we give a working multinomial--lognormal likelihood, exact in its readout model for direct measurement, and check its coverage by simulation. For paths of length $512$, mirroring reduces RMSE by a factor of $3$--$10$ for $H\ge0.5$, and with exact shares the nondecimated db2 estimator is $6$--$14\%$ above the root Cramér--Rao bound. Hadamard level tests are less accurate than direct measurement of the leading qubits at every budget. At $H=0.9$ and $2^{20}$ queries, maximum-likelihood amplitude estimation attains RMSE $0.0347$, against $0.0451$ for direct measurement and $0.0295$ with exact shares.

</details>


### [92] [Quantum Simulation of First-Quantised Chemistry with a Sum-of-Squares Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24885)
*Aleksei V. Ivanov,Christoph Sünderhauf,Jon Kunjummen,Nick S. Blunt,Róbert Izsák*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于非凸平方和哈密顿量表示与谱隙放大的第一量子化相位估计算法，在低量子比特、低填充度情形下比主流第二量子化算法最多节省16倍Toffoli门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子能量计算是量子计算的重要应用，但高效算法所需逻辑量子比特数庞大，扩展到数千逻辑比特工程难度极高；且多数算法只在轨道数约等于电子数的活性空间上评测，缺失动态相关所需的数倍轨道，真正化学模拟处于低填充度区间，而该区间有利于量子比特数随轨道数对数增长的第一量子化方法，但其在真实尺度轨道基组下是否具实用优势尚不明确。

Method: 面向通用轨道基组构造第一量子化相位估计（phase estimation）算法：以哈密顿量的非凸平方和（sum-of-squares）表示为核心，结合谱隙放大（spectral-gap amplification）技术；并在逻辑层面采用统一编译技术，与最先进的第二量子化算法进行公平对比。

Result: 在6个分子（4–24个电子、轨道数为电子数的5–14倍）上评测：低量子比特区间（仅数百逻辑比特可用）时第一量子化方法所需Toffoli门最多比第二量子化少16倍；优势可维持到数百至约1400个量子比特；最小量子比特数比第二量子化低1.4–2倍。

Conclusion: 在低量子比特、低填充度区间，所提第一量子化算法是轨道基组下量子化学模拟的首选方法。

Abstract: Calculating molecular energies is expected to be an important application of quantum computers, but the most efficient algorithms to achieve this typically have significant space requirements. Scaling quantum processors to thousands of logical qubits is a daunting engineering challenge, so developing algorithms that run with reduced qubit requirements is of particular importance. Moreover, most algorithms are benchmarked on active spaces with about as many orbitals as electrons, missing dynamical correlation which is necessary to describe real chemistry. Capturing this correlation requires several times more orbitals than electrons, even in compact atomic-orbital basis sets. This low-filling regime favours first quantisation, where each electron is represented by a number of qubits that grows only logarithmically with the number of orbitals. Whether this approach translates into a practical advantage in orbital basis sets of realistic size, however, has been unclear. Here, we develop a first-quantised phase estimation algorithm for generic orbital basis sets, based on a non-convex sum-of-squares representation of the Hamiltonian and spectral-gap amplification. We compare it with the state-of-the-art second-quantised algorithm, compiling both with the same techniques at the logical level. For six molecules with 4 to 24 electrons in 5 to 14 times as many orbitals, our first-quantised method needs up to 16 times fewer Toffoli gates than the second-quantised algorithm in the low-qubit regime, where only a few hundred logical qubits are available. It stays cheaper up to between several hundred and about 1\,400 qubits, and its smallest qubit count is 1.4 to 2 times lower than in second quantisation. In the low-qubit and low-filling regime, our algorithm is therefore the method of choice for chemistry in orbital basis sets.

</details>


### [93] [CREST: A Hardware-Aware GNN-Based Framework for High-Fidelity and Robust Superconducting Quantum Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24886)
*Wenjie Sun,Xiaoyu Li,Zhigang Wang,Yuchen He,Shengbin Wang,Hantao Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编译需为输入电路选择连通的执行区域，该选择约束后续路由并影响噪声；但仅靠聚合硬件质量指标难以可靠预测特定电路的执行保真度。同时，校准更新会改变区域适用性，并触发依赖校准的资源结构重建，需要一种不依赖重建、可复用且能直接预测电路级保真度的兼容性评估方法。

Method: CREST对电路与候选区域分别图编码：电路侧编码门依赖关系与逻辑量子比特交互，区域侧编码连通性与当前校准属性；通过双向跨图注意力关联两种表示；并以真实设备执行结果作为监督信号对候选区域进行排序训练。训练后的打分器在更新校准属性后可直接复用，无需重训练。

Result: 在天衍-176上，对来自七个数据集的324个电路评估，CREST相较最强基线平均测量分布保真度提升19.03%，并在每个数据集上均取得最高平均保真度；在使用更新校准属性、不重训练的情况下，同一模型在六个测量日期上保持最高总体平均保真度。

Conclusion: 统一的电路-区域兼容性模型CREST可在多样工作负载上提升执行保真度，并在硬件校准变化时持续保持该优势，验证了基于真实执行结果监督的GNN区域选择方法的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum compilation on superconducting processors requires selecting a connected execution region whose topology and calibrated properties suit the input circuit. This choice constrains subsequent routing and influences the noise encountered during execution, yet aggregate hardware-quality metrics alone may not reliably predict the execution fidelity of a given circuit. Calibration updates can also change region suitability and trigger the rebuilding of calibration-dependent resource structures. We present CREST, a graph neural network (GNN)-based framework that evaluates circuit-region compatibility before routing. It encodes gate dependencies and logical-qubit interactions alongside each candidate region's connectivity and current calibration attributes; bidirectional cross-graph attention relates the two representations, while real-device execution outcomes supervise candidate ranking. The trained scorer is reused with updated calibration attributes. Evaluations on 324 circuits from seven datasets on TianYan-176 show that CREST improves mean measurement-distribution fidelity by 19.03% over the strongest baseline and achieves the highest mean fidelity on every dataset. Furthermore, using updated calibration attributes, the same trained model maintains the highest overall mean fidelity across six measurement dates without retraining. With a unified model of circuit-region compatibility, CREST improves execution fidelity across diverse workloads and sustains that advantage as hardware calibration changes.

</details>


### [94] [Residual-Algebraic Design of Symmetry-Preserving Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24890)
*Sahinur Reja*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出对称保持（SP）变分量子线路虽把演化限制在物理相关子空间，但并不能保证该子空间内可达；残余动力学李代数的交换子（commutant）会把目标对称扇区进一步分成不变块，从而给出保真度的运动学上界。作者把这一诊断反转为线路设计准则：用残余交换子是否被打破、耦合哪些不变块、生成何种伴随模来筛选SP生成元，并在XXZ与Hubbard模型中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SP变分线路常被认为自动具备目标对称扇区内的通用性，但实际上受限生成元集合的动力学李代数可能更小，其交换子会将扇区再分解为不变块，导致块布居数守恒并限制可达态。作者希望先诊断这种“残余不可达性”，再将其转化为可操作的线路设计原则，避免盲目依赖变分优化。

Method: 1）计算受限生成元集合的动力学李代数及其交换子，识别对称扇区内的不变块与守恒块布居数，导出对目标态保真度的运动学上界。2）把诊断反转为筛选准则：考察候选SP生成元是否打破残余交换子、耦合哪些不变块、在残余代数下生成何种伴随模。3）对两个正交残余块，证明单个实型块间基本旋转即可生成整个正交代数。4）推导复逃逸判据：若块间种子的实部与虚部在残余 intertwiner 代数上线性无关，则生成模为完整复耦合空间，扩大后代数变为 special unitary。5）由连通的残余块图给出多块推广，并提出最小代价生成树式线路设计。6）在XXZ与Hubbard模型中做残余代数筛选并构造扩展SP线路。

Result: 残余代数筛选成功识别出XXZ与Hubbard模型中SP线路的可达性障碍，并指出能消除这些障碍的SP生成元扩展；理论上给出了保真度上界、单旋转生成正交代数、复逃逸导致su(N)型代数以及多块生成树构造等结论。

Conclusion: 对称保持本身不足以保证扇区内可达性；残余交换子提供了诊断保真度上界与筛选线路生成元的统一工具。由此形成的“残余对称性诊断—保真度界—预测性线路设计”路径，可在变分优化前低成本评估并改进SP线路，并在XXZ与Hubbard模型中得到了验证。

Abstract: Symmetry-preserving (SP) variational quantum circuits restrict evolution to physically relevant sectors of Hilbert space, but symmetry preservation alone does not guarantee accessibility within those sectors. We show that a restricted generator set can possess a residual dynamical Lie algebra whose commutant further decomposes the target symmetry sector into invariant blocks. The associated block populations are conserved and impose a kinematic upper bound on the fidelity with a target state. We then invert this diagnostic into a circuit-design principle: candidate SP generators can be screened before variational optimization by asking whether they break the residual commutant, which invariant blocks they couple, and what adjoint module they generate under the residual algebra. For two orthogonal residual blocks we prove that a single elementary real inter-block rotation generates the full orthogonal algebra. We further derive a complex escape criterion: if the real and imaginary parts of an inter-block seed are independent over the residual intertwiner algebra, the generated module is the full complex coupling space and the enlarged algebra becomes special unitary. A connected residual-block graph yields a natural multi-block generalization and suggests minimum-cost spanning-tree circuit design. We demonstrate this design principle in the XXZ and Hubbard models, where residual-algebra screening identifies SP circuit extensions that remove the relevant accessibility obstructions. These examples establish a unified route from residual-symmetry diagnosis and fidelity bounds to predictive quantum circuit design.

</details>

Abstract: We present the experimental preparation of a radio frequency (RF)-dressed-state qubit, the coherent operation of a single RF-dressed qubit, and the selective readout of RF-dressed hyperfine states of $^{171}$Yb${^+}$ ions trapped using a novel microstructured ion trap chip. We demonstrate the individual addressing of RF-dressed-state qubits in a two-ion Coulomb crystal. To this end, we developed, manufactured, and characterized a trap chip specifcally designed for quantum information processing with RF-dressed-state qubits. The chip consists of multiple layers, one of which includes an integrated resonator that creates a gradient of the RF magnetic field amplitude along the axis of a linear ion crystal. This RF gradient enables the individual addressing of dressed qubit states as well as their coupling. We characterize the ion trap chip experimentally using the hyperfine states of the electronic ground state of laser cooled $^{171}$Yb${^+}$ ions and by applying a single, global, modulated RF field oscillating near 12.6 GHz that drives dressed-state resonances.
  We present a microfabricated multilayer ion trap chip specifically designed for quantum information processing based on dressed-state qubits. This ion trap chip consists of multiple layers, one of which includes an integrated radio frequency (RF) resonator, creating a gradient of the RF magnetic field amplitude that allows for individual qubit addressing and qubit-qubit coupling. We experimentally characterize this ion trap chip demonstrating dressed-qubit state preparation, coherent single dressed-qubit operation, and dressed-state-selective read-out using hyperfine states of the electronic ground state of $^{171}$Yb${^+}$. For this purpose, a global modulated RF driving field oscillating near 12.6 GHz is applied to the ions. In addition, we demonstrate individual addressing of dressed-state qubits in a two-ion Coulomb crystal.

### [96] [A Nonsecular Quantum Master Equation with Detailed Balance and Second-order Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24911)
*Ji-Hui Pei,Jin-Fu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构造了一个满足时间反演KMS（TR-KMS）细致平衡的非久期量子主方程，在有条件下证明其约化密度矩阵与精确解之差时间一致地为O(λ²)，并在双耦合振子基准中横跨久期与非久期区均保持O(λ²)精度，优于现有细致平衡方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的开放量子系统理论应同时继承微观可逆性带来的细致平衡结构，并在整个弛豫过程中保持准确。但量子多体系统谱密集，即使在弱耦合下久期近似也会失效，二者难以兼顾。

Method: 在Redfield生成元基础上施加TR-KMS细致平衡，并令其在共振处匹配原生成元的耗散与相干系数，从而构造非久期主方程；证明时间一致的O(λ²)误差界（λ为系统-浴耦合强度），并指出TR-KMS细致平衡与共振匹配相容，而非时间反演版本不相容。

Result: 以两个耦合振子的精确微观动力学为基准，新方程在久期与非久期区均保持O(λ²)精度，优于所测试的既有细致平衡方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为从微观模型出发、超越久期近似地得到热力学一致的开放量子动力学描述提供了系统化路径。

Abstract: An effective theory of open quantum systems should preserve the detailed balance structure inherited from microscopic reversibility and remain accurate throughout relaxation. Meeting both requirements is particularly challenging in quantum many-body systems, where dense spectra can invalidate the secular approximation even at weak coupling. Here we construct a nonsecular quantum master equation that satisfies time-reversal Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (TR-KMS) detailed balance. Under suitable conditions, we prove a time-uniform $O(λ^2)$ bound on the difference between the predicted and exact reduced density matrices, where $λ$ is the system-bath coupling strength. Our construction completes the Redfield generator by imposing detailed balance while matching its dissipative and coherent coefficients at resonance. In this approach, TR-KMS detailed balance is compatible with resonance matching, whereas its counterpart without time reversal is not. In a benchmark against the exact microscopic dynamics of two coupled oscillators, our equation maintains $O(λ^2)$ accuracy across secular and nonsecular regimes, outperforming the existing detailed-balance equations tested. These results provide a systematic route from microscopic models to thermodynamically consistent descriptions of open quantum dynamics beyond the secular approximation.

</details>

### [97] [Retinal arterial velocity-proxy waveforms from differential spectral broadening in laser Doppler holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23599)
*Zacharie Auray,Yann Fischer,Olivier Martinache,Michael Atlan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出并验证一种基于激光多普勒全息（LDH）的视网膜动脉脉搏波形提取方法：用血管与局部背景谱宽之差（有符号差分展宽 Δσ）作为血流速度代理，可分辨真实动脉的搏动波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDH 能记录视网膜动脉中搏动性谱展宽，但谱宽同时受血管内血流与局部背景/仪器因素影响，缺乏一种简单、可量化的方式把“真实血流搏动”从背景波动中分离出来，并赋予其速度量纲解释。

Method: 对血管区域与局部背景分别测量多普勒谱宽，做差分得到有符号展宽波形 Δσ；用极简高斯卷积模型论证应采用二次（平方）相减以消除背景贡献；再用多普勒定标把附加谱宽换算为特征血流速度。以真实动脉与“虚构/虚设动脉”（imaginary arteries，同流程处理）作对照检验。

Result: 真实动脉呈现清晰的搏动性波形；经完全相同处理的对照（imaginary）血管仅表现为围绕零的低幅波动，说明 Δσ 中的搏动成分并非处理伪迹。经经验定标后的信号可解释为速度代理。

Conclusion: Δσ 可作为速度的代理量，用于刻画搏动波形形状并反映单次采集、稳定条件下同一血管内的相对变化；该方法简单、无需绝对定标即可做波形级分析，但不宜直接当作绝对速度测量。

Abstract: Laser Doppler holography (LDH) records pulsatile spectral broadening in retinal arteries. We compare vessel and local-background measurements to extract a signed differential-broadening waveform, $Δσ$. A minimal Gaussian convolution model motivates quadratic subtraction, and Doppler scaling relates the added width to a characteristic blood velocity. Real arteries show pulsatile waveforms, whereas identically processed imaginary arteries show only low-amplitude fluctuations around zero. We interpret the empirically scaled signal as a velocity proxy for waveform shape and within-vessel changes during a single acquisition under stable conditions.

</details>


### [98] [Optical Neural Networks Should Be Evaluated by Function, Not Only Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23610)
*Shi-Yuan Ma,Xingjian Bai,Ryan Hamerly,Mahmoud Jalali Mehrabad*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向功能的评估方法，将ONN中的光学模块视为实值张量之间的映射，通过界定边界、用数字模块替换以匹配性能、并按每次推理对比系统级指标，从而隔离光学贡献并提供数字基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光学神经网络（ONN）中，光学模块通常只接管数字ML流水线中的部分计算，而现有工作往往难以说明光学模块自身完成了多少任务、以及它对吞吐量或能效等系统级优势的实际贡献，缺乏统一评估口径。

Method: 提出三步函数导向评估方法：(i) 在流水线中界定光学模块边界，以输入输出实值张量表示；(ii) 用标准数字模块替换之，找出在同一任务与指标上性能匹配的数字模块；(iii) 针对匹配的数字模块，按每次推理报告速度、能耗等系统级声明。前两步汇总为"表达能力卡"（Expressivity Card），并在已发表的ONN系统上示范使用。方法不依赖物理细节。

Result: 通过在已发表ONN系统上的演示，证明该流程可有效隔离光学模块的贡献，并为其系统级优势声称提供具体、可比的数字参考基准。

Conclusion: 该评估框架不仅适用于光学系统，也适用于任何在ML中接管部分数字流水线的物理模拟计算平台，为跨平台公平比较提供了通用方法论。

Abstract: Optical neural networks (ONNs) are attracting significant attention as alternative platforms for machine learning (ML). In an ONN, an optical module often takes over part of the computation inside a digital ML pipeline. How much of the task does the optical module itself perform, and how much does it contribute to any claimed system-level advantages, such as throughput or energy?
  This paper offers a simple, function-oriented evaluation methodology. Whatever its internal physics, the optical module appears to the surrounding digital pipeline as a mapping between real-valued tensors. We therefore evaluate it in three steps: (i) define its boundary in the pipeline, as real-valued tensors in and out; (ii) replace it with standard digital modules to identify those that match the optical system's performance on the same task and metric; and (iii) state system-level claims, such as speed or energy, per inference against the performance-matched digital module. We summarize steps (i) and (ii) in an Expressivity Card and demonstrate its use on published ONN systems. This helps isolate the optical module's contribution and provides a concrete digital reference for assessing its claimed system-level advantages, without the need to understand the physics.
  While we focus on optical systems as the most prominent example, the same approach applies to any physical analog computing platform used in ML, wherever a physical module takes over part of a digital pipeline.

</details>


### [99] [Simultaneous spectral measurement and programmable filtering on a universal silicon photonic chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23667)
*Yangyang Wan,Rui Tang,Kazuhiro Ikeda,Makoto Okano,Kasidit Toprasertpong,Mitsuru Takenaka*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一款通用硅光子芯片，在单一集成架构内统一实现光谱测量与可编程滤波，实测光谱分辨率0.05 nm、带宽超过100 nm，并可并行探测8路独立激光源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上光谱测量与光谱滤波依赖两套独立光学系统实现，难以在共同平台上同时、可编程地协同工作，缺乏集成化的通用方案。

Method: 芯片由两个8×8可编程幺正变换电路经光强调制器互连，构成通用线性光学回路；区别于惯用的等长互连，作者故意在两个幺正电路之间引入不等光程，利用随波长变化的相位演化产生光谱多样性，同时保持通用回路的可编程性，从而实现光谱测量与可编程滤波的并行运作。

Result: 制备芯片在超过100 nm带宽内实现0.05 nm光谱分辨率；可同时测量8个独立激光源；可生成多种可编程光谱滤波函数；并在滤波器函数动态重构过程中同时完成光谱测量与滤波。

Conclusion: 结果确立了通用光子回路作为在单芯片上集成光谱传感与可编程光学处理的通用平台。

Abstract: Spectral measurement and spectral filtering are two fundamental operations in optics, yet they are conventionally implemented using separate optical systems, hindering their simultaneous and programmable operation on a common platform. Here, we demonstrate a universal silicon photonic chip that unifies spectral measurement and programmable filtering within a single integrated architecture. The chip comprises two 8 * 8 programmable unitary transformation circuits interconnected through optical intensity modulators, forming a universal linear optical circuit. In contrast to conventional universal circuits employing equal-length interconnections, we deliberately introduce different optical path lengths between the two unitary circuits. The resulting wavelength-dependent phase evolution provides spectral diversity while preserving the programmability of the universal circuit, enabling parallel spectral measurement and programmable filtering. The fabricated chip achieves a spectral resolution of 0.05 nm over a bandwidth exceeding 100 nm, simultaneously measures eight independent laser sources, and generates multiple programmable spectral filter functions. We further demonstrate simultaneous spectral measurement and filtering during dynamic reconfiguration of the filter functions. These results establish universal photonic circuits as a versatile platform for integrating spectral sensing and programmable optical processing on a single chip.

</details>


### [100] [Observation of Exact Mobility-Edge Phases in Two-Dimensional Photonic Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23823)
*Chaohua Wu,Yingying Shi,Juan Kang,Qinglong Zhang,Shuo Liu,Gang Chen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作通过在二维Aubry-André准晶中引入辅助子系统，利用等谱约化揭示隐藏的自对偶结构，获得精确迁移率边条件，并在光子波导晶格中实验实现了扩展态、局域态与部分局域态的分区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确迁移率边已在一维准周期模型中确立，但二维体系中的实现仍基本空白；二维因缺乏可解析处理的自对偶结构而难以给出精确迁移率边。

Method: 在二维Aubry-André准晶中引入辅助子系统，通过等谱约化（isospectral reduction）导出有效耦合的隐藏自对偶结构，得到精确迁移率边条件；调节辅助结构以调控有效耦合与迁移率边构型，辅助共振进一步支持平带态；在装饰Lieb型准晶的光子波导晶格中借助位点选择性淬火动力学探测能量依赖的局域化。

Result: 获得解析的精确迁移率边，可将谱划分为扩展、局域与部分局域区域；辅助结构可裁剪迁移率边构型，辅助共振产生平带态；光子波导实验验证了预测相图及能量依赖局域化行为。

Conclusion: 辅助子系统+等谱约化为二维体系精确迁移率边提供了通用路线，构成调控局域化的通用平台，可推广至其他物理平台与更高维体系。

Abstract: Mobility edges, separating localized and extended states in energy, are central to localization physics. While exact mobility edges have been established in several one-dimensional quasiperiodic models, their realization in two dimensions remains largely unexplored. Here, we uncover a general route to exact mobility edges in the two-dimensional Aubry-Andre quasicrystal by introducing auxiliary subsystems. Through isospectral reduction, we reveal a hidden self-dual structure that yields exact mobility-edge conditions, separating extended, localized, and partially localized states in the spectrum. Tailoring the auxiliary structures controls the effective couplings and resulting mobility edge configurations, while auxiliary resonances further support flat-band states. In a decorated Lieb-type quasicrystal, we experimentally realize these predicted phases in photonic waveguide lattices and probe their energy-dependent localization through site-selective quench dynamics. Our results provide a versatile platform for controlling localization and can be readily extended to other physical platforms and higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [101] [In Situ Training of Optical Neural Networks by Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23875)
*Ilker Oguz,Colin Christian,Christophe Moser,Demetri Psaltis,Gordon Wetzstein*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出"optics as code"：让编码智能体仅凭测量数据直接在光学硬件上训练光学神经网络，无需逐器件标定或硬件梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学神经网络虽快且节能，但器件偏离设计值、必须硬件在环训练；现有原位训练依赖专家构建的标定数字模型，或依赖无梯度的试错法，成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 构建一个harness，向编码智能体（会编写并运行程序的LLM）返回其探测的相机图像，并要求每次请求附带一个假设；智能体据此形成对光学的物理理解，并用可解释的光学元件对网络进行重参数化，在硬件上调优。

Result: 在分类与图像编码任务上优于无梯度方法，接近通过标定模型的反向传播效果，而成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 无需逐器件标定、无需硬件梯度，且随模型能力提升而改进，为大规模可扩展光学神经网络提供实用路径，并可作为标定与优化光学实验的通用工具。

Abstract: Optical neural networks promise fast, energy-efficient computing, but every device departs from its design and must be trained with the hardware. In situ training relies on an expert-built calibrated digital model of each device or on gradient-free trial and error. Coding agents, large language models that write and run programs, have shown an emergent ability to understand and edit the weights of digital neural networks. Here we introduce optics as code, a method in which a coding agent trains an optical neural network from measurements alone, through a harness that returns camera images of its probes and requires a hypothesis per request. We discovered that the agent forms a physical understanding of the optics and reparametrizes the network with interpretable optical elements tuned on the hardware. It outperforms gradient-free methods in classification and image encoding, approaching backpropagation through a calibrated model at a fraction of the cost. Needing neither per-device calibration nor gradients through the hardware, and improving with more capable models, optics as code offers a practical route to large, scalable optical neural networks and a general tool for calibrating and optimizing optical experiments.

</details>


### [102] [Analysis of the Second-Harmonic Generation Process in Nonlinear Metasurfaces Based on Multiple Quantum Wells: The Approach of Nonlinear Current Density, and The Development of Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23943)
*Sepehrdad Bahrami*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种半解析框架分析基于多量子阱(MQW)的非线性等离激元超表面二次谐波产生,包含非线性电流密度建模、饱和效应、SH场周期边界条件,并在8μm波长下设计结构,于23.5 kW/cm²入射强度实现0.02%转换效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离激元谐振器可增强MQW区域内基频场强度,从而强化子带间跃迁,显著提升SHG效率;但缺乏能同时考虑MQW饱和效应与周期结构中SH场边界条件的准确分析模型,需要建立可靠的理论/半解析方法来指导器件设计。

Method: 1) 建立MQW区域内作为SH辐射源的非线性电流密度公式,并计入限制非线性响应的饱和效应;2) 推导周期结构中SH场的边界条件,定义SH场的周期边界条件;3) 将上述要素整合为半解析计算框架,并用已报道实验数据验证;4) 针对8 μm基频波长设计高效MQW结构与等离激元超表面。

Result: 所提框架经文献实验数据验证;设计的中红外(8 μm)非线性超表面在23.5 kW/cm²入射强度下实现0.02%的转换效率。

Conclusion: 该半解析方法可有效刻画含饱和效应的MQW非线性超表面SHG过程,适用于中红外波段等离激元增强SHG器件的定量分析与设计优化。

Abstract: Using plasmonic metasurfaces integrated with nonlinear materials, such as multiple quantum wells (MQWs), has been an excellent solution to enhance nonlinear light-matter interactions. In this approach, a plasmonic resonator enhances the intensity of the fundamental harmonic within the MQWs region, increasing the strength of the intersubband transitions inside the MQWs. As a result, the efficiency of second harmonic generation (SHG) increases significantly. In this paper, we present a new approach to analyze the SHG process in nonlinear metasurfaces based on MQWs. In this approach, first, we formulate the nonlinear current density within the MQW region, which serves as the radiation source of the second harmonic wave. Notably, the saturation effect in MQWs, that limits the nonlinear response, is accounted for. Second, we address the development of boundary conditions for the second harmonic (SH) field in periodic structures, which ultimately leads us to define periodic boundary conditions for the SH field. Third, we combine these elements into a semi-analytical framework and validate it against previously reported experimental data. Finally, an efficient MQWs structure and plasmonic metasurface are designed for the fundamental wavelength of 8 $μ$m. After implementing the proposed algorithm, we observe that the designed nonlinear metasurface achieves a significant conversion efficiency of 0.02% under the incident intensity of 23.5 kW/cm$^2$.
  KEYWORDS: Second harmonic generation, metasurface, multiple quantum wells, nonlinear current density, periodic boundary conditions

</details>


### [103] [Scattering features of a self-dual spectral singularity in coupled ring resonators with balanced gain and loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23959)
*Jeng Yi Lee*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于互易性与有效PT对称传输矩阵的参数化方法，给出双环谐振器耦合直波导的完整相图，并揭示在特定耦合系数—衰减因子关系与回音壁共振条件下出现自对偶谱奇异（CPAL），此时系统统一表现为透射消光、CPAL与损耗环侧单向无反射；减弱增益/损耗会压缩CPAL带宽，且透射消光可远离共振。解析结果由电磁仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一个增益环与一个等量（互易配对、有效传播常数相同）损耗环构成的双环—直波导耦合结构的普遍散射行为，厘清增益/损耗、耦合与共振共同决定的散射相图与奇异点条件。

Method: 要求两环的单圈振幅衰减与增益因子构成互易对、有效传播常数相同；由互易性与有效PT对称传输矩阵出发，采用参数化方法构建通用相图以划分散射输出；解析推导CPAL（自对偶谱奇异）形成条件，并与电磁仿真对照验证。

Result: CPAL出现在耦合系数与衰减因子的特定关系及共振回音壁模式下；减小两环增益/损耗强度会收窄CPAL对应的光谱带宽；凡满足CPAL参数的系统均呈现相同散射特征——对入射波长而言的透射消光、CPAL及损耗环侧单向无反射；透射消光也可出现在偏离共振处；电磁仿真验证了解析预测。

Conclusion: 该双环PT对称耦合系统具有由耦合与衰减因子关系标定的统一散射相图，CPAL为其中的自对偶谱奇异点；所得判据与带宽、非共振消光等结论可为片上增益—损耗调控的无反射/相干吸收器件设计提供依据。

Abstract: We theoretically and numerically discuss the general scattering property of a coupled two-ring-resonators externally coupled to a straight bus waveguide. Specifically, we require one ring resonator to be light field amplifying and another to be that dissipative, where the associated attenuation and gain factors for a field amplitude after one-round trip are formed in a reciprocal pair as well as the effective wave propagation constants are same. Obtained from reciprocity and an effective PT-symmetric transfer matrix, we exploit parametrization to propose a general phase diagram, identifying overall scattering outcomes. Especially, we find that the formation of a self-dual spectral singularity, namely coherent perfect absorption-lasing (CPAL), is occurred at a certain relation of a coupling coefficient and an attenuation factor as well as resonant whispering gallery modes. Reducing light amplification and dissipation strengths in each rings narrows the spectral bandwidth associated with CPAL. We further discover that any system with the CPAL parameters would exhibit a same scattering feature: transmission extinction, CPAL, and uni-directional reflectionless at the loss ring side, with respect to an incident wavelength. Additionally, a transmission extinction can be away from the resonance. These analytical predictions are verified by electromagnetic simulations.

</details>


### [104] [Gas-induced reversible spectral tuning of bound states in the continuum in perovskite metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24096)
*Nikita Glebov,Yuliya Kenzhebayeva,Pavel Pankin,Mikhail Masharin,Martin Rojas Bustamante,Xiuzhen Liu,Shixiong Li,Ruslan Azizov,Dmitrii Maksimov,Hatice Altug,Andrey Bogdanov,Sergey Makarov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用卤化物钙钛矿（MAPbBr₃）超表面中的连续域束缚态（BIC）共振，通过HCl/HBr气相阴离子交换实现完全可逆、连续、>40 nm的可见光谱调谐，并演示了BIC激光的连续可逆调谐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIC为紧凑光子结构中的光学共振工程提供了强有力途径，可用于传感、非线性光学、调制与激光。但走向实用BIC超构光学，关键一步是制备后的动态可逆光谱调控。现有手段存在局限：光/电光调谐折射率变化有限，热光方法需持续外部驱动，相变材料多为离散开关而非连续重构。

Method: 采用纳米压印光刻制备MAPbBr₃钙钛矿超表面，在可见光区支持BIC衍生共振。以HCl蒸气触发阴离子交换反应：Cl⁻扩散进入钙钛矿晶格并部分取代Br⁻，使电子带隙单调增大、激子共振蓝移（分钟量级），折射率下降从而调谐BIC共振；再用HBr蒸气逆转交换过程恢复初始谱位。

Result: BIC共振在可见光区实现>40 nm的连续调谐，且经HBr处理后完全可逆地回到初始光谱位置；作为功能演示，实现了同一超表面上BIC介导激光的连续、可逆光谱调谐。

Conclusion: 气相阴离子交换是一种非破坏性机制，可对钙钛矿超表面实现连续、可逆、大范围的制备后调控，为化学可编程激光、自适应超构光学与有源纳米光子器件提供新途径。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) provide a powerful route to engineering optical resonances in compact photonic structures, enabling enhanced light--matter interaction for sensing, nonlinear optics, optical modulation, and lasing. The next step towards practical BIC-based meta-optics is dynamic and reversible spectral control after fabrication. However, optical and electro-optical tuning typically produces limited refractive-index changes, thermo-optical approaches require continuous external actuation, and phase-change materials often provide discrete switching rather than continuous spectral reconfiguration. Here we demonstrate giant, fully reversible, and continuous spectral tuning of BIC resonances in halide-perovskite metasurfaces. Using nanoimprint lithography, we fabricate MAPbBr$_3$ metasurfaces, that support BIC-derived resonances in the visible spectral range. Exposure to HCl vapor initiates an anion-exchange reaction in which Cl$^-$ ions diffuse into the perovskite crystalline lattice and partially replace Br$^-$ ions, producing a monotonic increase of the electronic bandgap and blueshift of the excitonic resonance on a minute timescale. This leads to a decrease in the refractive index and tunes the BIC resonance by more than 40~nm in the visible range. Subsequent exposure to HBr vapor reverses the exchange process and restores the resonance to its initial spectral position. As a functional demonstration, we show continuous reversible spectral tuning of BIC-mediated lasing from the same metasurface. Our results establish gas-phase anion exchange as a non-destructive mechanism for continuous, reversible, and large-range post-fabrication control of perovskite metasurfaces, opening opportunities for chemically programmable lasers, adaptive meta-optics, and active nanophotonic devices.

</details>


### [105] [Semiclassical Quantum Mechanics and the Optics of Time-Varying Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24291)
*S. A. R. Horsley,R. Sokol,E. -M. Graefe*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将光波在时变介质中的频谱演化理论与半经典量子力学建立联系：不同微观模型等价于算符排序选择，波传播由非厄米薛定谔方程支配，并可导出推广的哈密顿方程；据此设计了在频率与时间上"聚焦"波的色散时间调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速、高对比度地调制折射率等光学参数可显著重塑波的频谱，频率与时间算符不对易，其数学结构与量子力学中位置-动量算符类似。作者希望厘清该理论同半经典量子力学的关系，并简化过去对时间反射可忽略介质所采用的过于复杂的计算方法。

Method: 建立波传播与非厄米薛定谔方程及半经典量子力学的对应关系：(i) 论证不同时变材料微观模型等价于不同算符排序方案；(ii) 导出支配波传播的非厄米薛定谔方程；(iii) 取半经典极限，得到推广的哈密顿方程，用以描述波包中心频率与到达时间在调制介质中的演化。

Result: 给出推广的哈密顿方程并验证其有效性；利用该理论设计色散时间调制，实现对波在频率与时间上的"聚焦"，其行为类比于半经典量子力学中的焦散。同时表明在时间反射效应可忽略时，可用更简洁的框架替代原有复杂计算。

Conclusion: 研究深化了经典波传播与量子力学之间的联系，并提供了一套简洁有力的半经典工具，可用于分析与设计时变介质中的波调控（如频率-时间聚焦与焦散），避免不必要的复杂计算。

Abstract: Rapid, high contrast modulation of optical parameters such as the refractive index can radically reshape a wave's frequency spectrum as it propagates. Non-commuting frequency and time operators can be used to describe this reshaping, analogous to the position and momentum operators of quantum mechanics. Here we develop the connection between this theory and semi-classical quantum mechanics, showing that: (i) different microscopic models of time-varying materials are equivalent to choices of operator ordering; (ii) wave propagation is governed by an unusual non-Hermitian Schrödinger equation; (iii) we can take a semi-classical limit, finding a generalization of Hamilton's equations governing the central frequency and arrival time of a wave spectrum as it propagates through a modulated material. The theory can be applied to design dispersive time modulations that `focus' a wave in frequency and time, analogous to caustics in semiclassical quantum mechanics. Our results not only deepen the connection between classical wave propagation and quantum mechanics, but can also simplify overly complex calculations that have been previously applied to media where time reflection effects are negligible.

</details>


### [106] [Delay Mismatches Shape the Spectra of Three Synchronized Semiconductor Lasers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24306)
*Sergi Torres-Serra,Mohammadreza Mokhtari,Cristina Masoller,Jordi Tiana-Alsina,Apostolos Argyris,Miguel C. Soriano*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在三台同步混沌激光器构成的网络中，射频强度涨落谱的峰位与形态强烈依赖于被同步的激光组合；激光器间延时失配的倒数定义特征频率，当多个谐波贡献在频率上重叠时产生相长增强的谱峰，实验与数值模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三激光同步网络中，不同同步构型下射频谱差异的物理来源，特别是各激光器反馈与耦合延时并不完全相同（存在延时失配）时，延时失配对同步动力学频率成分的决定作用。

Method: 构建三台混沌激光器的耦合网络（参数近似但不完全相同，含各自的反馈延时与耦合延时），测量不同激光组合同步时的强度涨落射频谱；对比两种三激光构型，利用延时失配的倒数提取特征频率，并用相同延时配置进行数值模拟与实验比对。

Result: 谱峰的位置与形态随同步激光集合显著变化；每对激光器间延时失配的倒数引入塑造谱响应的特征频率；当多个谐波贡献在频率上重叠时出现谱峰，表现为谱功率的相长增强；数值模拟在峰位与整体谱结构上与实验测量明确吻合。

Conclusion: 同步动力学的频率成分主要由延时失配网络及其谐波决定，而非对称耦合结构；延时失配构成可设计谱响应的关键自由度。

Abstract: We study a network of three synchronized chaotic lasers and the radio frequency spectra of the intensity fluctuations. The position and morphology of the peaks are significantly different depending on the set of lasers being synchronized. The lasers and coupling paths are similar but not identical in all parameters, including their feedback and coupling delay times. The inverse of the delay mismatch between each pair of lasers introduces characteristic frequencies that shape the spectral response of the synchronized system. By studying two different three-laser configurations, we find that peaks appear whenever multiple harmonic contributions overlap in frequency, resulting in constructive enhancement of the spectral power. Numerical simulations performed with the same delay configurations show a clear agreement with the experimental measurements, reproducing both the peak positions and the overall spectral structure. These results indicate that the frequency content of the synchronized dynamics is largely determined by the delay-mismatch network and its harmonics.

</details>


### [107] [Bound States in the Continuum as Converging Scattering Zeros in Metasurfaces: Why Accidental BICs Require Nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24326)
*Karim Achouri,Youssef Dib,Hossein Allahverdizadeh,Sergejs Boroviks,Mahmoud Elsawy*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将连续域束缚态(BIC)与超表面的反射/透射零点联系起来：把响应分解为偶/奇分量并考察极点-零点相互作用，指出高Q共振表现为两个反射零点之间的透射零点（或反之），BIC对应散射零点的收敛；由此揭示被动局域偶极模型下斜入射时偶然BIC不可实现，需负振幅（高阶多极或晶格耦合），即偶然BIC本质非局域，并用硅脊超表面的空间色散模型加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIC可使超表面获得极高品质因数，但其如何产生、以及能否用有效材料参数描述仍不清楚，本文针对这一问题展开研究。

Method: 用有效磁化率描述超表面，通过反射、透射零点建模BIC；将响应分解为偶、奇部分，分析对应极点与零点的相互作用；并在被动局域偶极模型框架下讨论斜入射情形，最后以硅脊超表面为例，采用被动且具空间色散的模型进行仿真验证。

Result: 高Q共振表现为夹在两个反射零点之间的透射零点（或反之），零点间距由偶/奇共振线宽决定，BIC即零点收敛；该图像可解释对称保护BIC及其向准BIC的转变；但在被动局域偶极模型中，斜入射下偶然BIC要求某一磁化率共振具有负振幅（按Foster定理意味着有源性），该负振幅实为局域模型无法刻画的高阶多极或晶格耦合的标志，表明偶然BIC本质上是非局域的；空间色散模型可复现并预测硅脊超表面响应，并描述破缺镜像对称使偶然BIC转为准BIC的过程。

Conclusion: BIC可由散射零点的收敛统一描述；被动局域偶极模型无法正确描述偶然BIC，其所需负振幅揭示偶然BIC的内禀非局域性，必须借助含空间色散（高阶多极、晶格耦合）的模型才能准确刻画，包括镜面对称破缺导致的准BIC转化。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) allow metasurfaces to achieve extremely high quality factors, yet it remains unclear how they emerge and how they can be described by effective material parameters. We address this question by modeling BICs through the reflection and transmission zeros of a metasurface described by effective susceptibilities. By decomposing its response into even and odd parts, and inspecting the interaction of the corresponding poles and zeros, we show that a high-Q resonance appears as a transmission zero squeezed between two reflection zeros, or vice versa. The separation between these scattering zeros is set by the linewidth of the corresponding even or odd resonance, so that a BIC corresponds to a convergence of these zeros. This simple concept explains symmetry-protected BICs and their transformation into quasi-BICs. However, it also reveals a fundamental limitation for accidental BICs: in a passive local dipolar model, the zeros cannot converge at oblique incidence unless one of the susceptibility resonances has a negative amplitude, which would make the metasurface active according to Foster's theorem. We demonstrate that this negative amplitude is in fact the signature of higher-order multipoles or lattice coupling effects that the local model cannot properly capture, which shows that accidental BICs are intrinsically nonlocal. We confirm this conclusion by simulating a silicon-ridge metasurface, whose response is reproduced and predicted by a passive, spatially dispersive model, which also describes how breaking the mirror symmetry of the metasurface turns the accidental BIC into a quasi-BIC.

</details>


### [108] [First Implementation and Characterization of a Fiber Amplifier in a Gravitational Wave Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24465)
*Margherita Turconi,Marina Trad Nery,Rémi Soulard,Matthieu Gosselin,Piernicola Spinicelli,Suzanne Melo,Camilla De Rossi,Antonino Chiummo,Mourad Merzougui,Jean-Pierre Coulon,Nelson Christensen,Walid Chaibi*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 首次将光纤放大器作为高功率激光源集成进Virgo引力波探测器的注入系统，实现130 W输出并满足频率噪声、功率噪声与光束指向噪声要求，累计运行超过17,000小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波干涉仪的灵敏度直接取决于注入激光的功率水平与噪声特性；光纤放大器在功率放大和光束质量方面具有潜力，被视为下一代探测器的候选技术，但此前尚缺乏在真实探测器系统中的实际部署验证。

Method: 在Virgo注入系统中引入光纤放大器作为高功率单频激光源，测量其130 W输出光束的频率噪声、相对功率噪声与光束指向噪声，并评估使Virgo探测器调试得以进行的稳频/稳功率锁定环路的性能。

Result: 输出130 W光束满足各项噪声指标要求；稳定环路性能支持Virgo探测器调试；系统累计连续运行超过17,000小时，是面向引力波探测的高功率单频光纤放大器迄今最长的连续表征与运行记录。

Conclusion: 验证了光纤放大器在实际引力波探测器注入链路中作为高功率激光源的可行性，为其在下一代探测器中的应用提供了长期的运行与表征依据。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave detectors rely on large-scale interferometers operating at exquisite sensitivities, where the laser source plays a central and critical role. The sensitivity of these instruments is directly influenced by the power level and noise properties of the injected light. Fiber amplifiers are a promising technology that are currently considered for the next generation of detectors because of their potential in laser power scaling and their superior output beam quality. In the context of the Virgo gravitational wave detector, we report the first implementation of a fiber amplifier into Virgo's injection system as a high-power laser source. We demonstrate a 130 W output beam that fulfills the requirements in terms of frequency noise, power noise, and beam-pointing noise. Additionally, we also show the performance of the stabilization loops that enable the commissioning of the Virgo detector. With more than 17,000 hours of operation, this work represents the longest continuous characterization and operation of a high-power and single-frequency fiber amplifier developed for gravitational-wave detection.

</details>


### [109] [Mid-Infrared Integrated Acousto-Optic Phase Modulation on GaAs/AlGaAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24530)
*Thomas Gérodou,Mathilde Urbain,Luca Lucia,Etienne Herth,Jean-René Coudevylle,Martina Morassi,Aristide Lemaître,Damien Teyssieux,Raffaele Colombelli,Adel Bousseksou,Mathieu Jeannin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于GaAs/AlGaAs异质结，演示了工作于460 MHz的中红外(7.5–10 μm)集成声光相位调制器，利用深蚀刻亚波长光栅同时约束表面声波与导波光，实现单片集成的电声换能与声光互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中红外集成声光器件缺乏同时具备宽带透明与压电特性的材料平台；且中红外光波长与声波波长相仿，难以同时传播并约束表面声波与导波光。

Method: 采用GaAs/AlGaAs异质结实现电声换能与声光互作用的单片集成；用深蚀刻亚波长光栅同时约束表面声波与光波；建立定量仿真框架进行器件设计，用外差式激光测振仪表征声学特性，并将器件嵌入集成马赫-曾德尔干涉仪进行定量性能分析。

Result: 仿真与实验结果高度一致，验证了所提出的设计流程；器件在7.5–10 μm波段实现460 MHz声光相位调制。

Conclusion: GaAs基平台结合亚波长光栅约束方案可获得可靠的中红外集成声光器件，为向更长波长中红外拓展的改进型集成声光器件铺平道路。

Abstract: We demonstrate a mid-infrared (7.5-10 um) integrated acousto-optic phase modulator operating at 460MHz based on a GaAs/AlGaAs heterostructure. The combination of broad mid-infrared transparency and piezoelectric properties of GaAs based structures allows monolithic integration of all the device functions (electro-acoustic transduction and acousto-optic interaction) on a single material platform. Addressing the challenge presented by comparable mid-infrared and acoustic wavelengths, simultaneous propagation and confinement of surface acoustic waves and guided light is achieved using a deep-etched sub-wavelength grating. We develop a comprehensive and quantitative simulation framework to design the modulator devices, characterize their acoustic properties in a heterodyne vibrometer and perform a full, quantitative analysis of the device performance by inserting them in an integrated Mach-Zehnder interferometer. Remarkable agreement with simulations validates the proposed design flow, and opens the way to improved integrated acousto-optic devices operating in the longer end of the mid-infrared.

</details>


### [110] [Generation of neutron Bessel beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24655)
*Owen Lailey,Dusan Sarenac,Nir Emelianenko,Charles W. Clark,David G. Cory,Huseyin Ekinci,Michael G. Huber,Jeremy W. Paster,Anna Sokolova,Diego Alba Venero,Dmitry A. Pushin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文通过在中子波前上刻印纳米级锥形相位梯度，实验实现了零阶、三阶和六阶中子贝塞尔光束，实现了对无质量粒子OAM与径向结构的直接调控，并提出了中子贝塞尔光栅干涉仪方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物质波可呈现类似光的结构化波场，为控制大质量粒子的空间与角动量自由度提供新手段。贝塞尔光束具有无衍射传播、自修复和确定轨道角动量（OAM）等特性，但此前尚未在中子上实验实现，缺乏相应的实验基础与干涉测量平台。

Method: 在中子波前上刻印纳米级锥形相位梯度以产生贝塞尔光束；通过引入方位角相位梯度分别生成零阶、三阶和六阶贝塞尔光束；并设计方案：中子贝塞尔光栅干涉仪，通过改变径向光栅周期来调控古伊（Gouy）相位。

Result: 成功实验生成零阶、三阶与六阶中子贝塞尔光束，直接实现了对OAM和径向结构的控制；所提干涉仪中，径向光栅周期改变约100 nm即可产生π的受控古伊相移。

Conclusion: 中子贝塞尔光束可作为衍射式中子聚焦的新平台，并为中子散射、衰变及OAM表征的理论研究提供实验基础；该干涉仪与已有光学和电子干涉方法形成互补。

Abstract: Matter waves can exhibit structured wavefields analogous to those of light, offering new means of controlling the spatial and angular-momentum degrees of freedom of massive particles. Bessel beams are a fundamental class of structured waves that exhibit non-diffracting propagation, self-healing, and well-defined orbital angular momentum (OAM). Here, we experimentally realize neutron Bessel beams by imprinting a nanoscale conical phase gradient onto a neutron wavefront. By incorporating azimuthal phase gradients, we generate zeroth-, third-, and sixth-order Bessel beams, demonstrating direct control of both the OAM and radial structure of a massive particle. We also propose a neutron Bessel-grating interferometer, complementary to established optical and electron interferometric methods, in which varying the radial grating period by approximately 100 nm yields a controlled Gouy phase shift of $π$. These results establish neutron Bessel beams as a new platform for diffractive neutron focusing and provide an experimental foundation for theoretical studies of Bessel beams in neutron scattering, decay, and OAM characterization.

</details>


### [111] [Raman-induced Kerr effect and heating in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24856)
*C. D. Cruz,J. C. Stephenson,J. K. Wahlstrand*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文用相位敏感的非简并泵浦-探测光谱在1 μm附近完整表征了金刚石偏振相关的三阶非线性极化率χ⁽³⁾，包括拉曼贡献，并测量了拉曼双光子吸收导致的绝对热沉积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石的各向异性χ⁽³⁾（Kerr与拉曼贡献）文献值分散、缺乏完整偏振分辨的定量表征，需要一种能直接、绝对测量全张量响应并可与低频ISRS测量互补的方法。

Method: 采用相位敏感非简并泵浦-探测光谱（近1 μm），测量探测脉冲随偏振与波长的频域相移和幅度变化，直接提取各向异性χ⁽³⁾全响应；并利用连续激光的时间分辨光束偏转测量泵浦-探测差频匹配声子共振时拉曼双光子吸收产生的热沉积。

Result: 拉曼组分的双光束耦合偏振依赖性与单光束选择定则的预言不符；Kerr系数落在文献报道范围内，各向异性参数满足Kleinman对称；拉曼极化率经色散标度后与514.5 nm布里渊参考测量一致，但低于绝对拉曼截面测量值；并直接测得金刚石中的热沉积。

Conclusion: 该技术可实现精确的绝对χ⁽³⁾测量，为定量受激拉曼加热显微术奠定基础，并有望扩展至热导率计量；适用于声子频率较高的其他材料。

Abstract: We fully characterize the polarization-dependent third-order nonlinear susceptibility in a diamond crystal using phase-sensitive nondegenerate pump-probe spectroscopy near 1~$μ$m wavelength. Measuring the frequency-domain phase shift and amplitude change of the probe pulse as a function of polarization and probe wavelength allows direct extraction of the full anisotropic $χ^{(3)}$ response, including the Raman contribution. We find that the polarization dependence of the two-beam coupling contribution for the Raman component differs from that predicted solely using one-beam selection rules. We measure a Kerr coefficient that is within the range of reported values in the literature, and our measured anisotropy parameters satisfy Kleinman symmetry, as expected. Our measured Raman polarizability agrees, after scaling for its dispersion, with a Brillouin-referenced measurement at 514.5~nm [Grimsditch and Ramdas, Phys. Rev. B \textbf{11}, 3139 (1975)], but is lower than measurements from absolute Raman cross sections. The same technique could be used to measure other materials with relatively high phonon frequencies, and it complements measurements of the Kerr contribution of low-frequency modes using impulsive stimulated Raman scattering (ISRS). When the pump-probe difference frequency matches the phonon resonance, Raman-enabled two-photon absorption occurs; we directly measure the resulting heat deposition in diamond using time-resolved beam deflection of a continuous-wave laser. The measurement technique demonstrated here is capable of precise absolute $χ^{(3)}$ measurements and will enable quantitative stimulated Raman heating microscopy, with potential extensions to thermal conductivity metrology.

</details>


### [112] [Time-Varying Medium from an Ensemble of Laser-Dressed Quantum Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24861)
*Adrián Juan-Delgado,Diego Martín-Cano,Paloma A. Huidobro*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一个含泵浦机制的、频率色散且时间调制介质的极简量子力学模型，基于被泵浦激光缀饰的量子发射体集合，推导宏观极化率并用Floquet方法求解Maxwell方程，获得了区别于忽略调制源的常规分析的丰富能带结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间调制介质分析常忽略调制源本身，导致对色散与时变响应耦合的描述不完整；作者希望厘清调制源在时变介质中的作用，并为利用驱动量子系统实现色散时变介质铺路。

Method: 构建由均匀量子发射体集合被泵浦激光缀饰的极简模型；计算其对微扰探测场的色散、时变响应，导出系综的宏观极化率，再结合Floquet形式求解Maxwell方程得到能带结构。

Result: 得到丰富的能带结构，其来源于色散极化激元响应与时间变化响应的相互作用，明显区别于忽略调制源的常规分析结果。

Conclusion: 工作阐明了调制源在时变介质中的角色，并为从驱动量子系统实现色散时变介质提供了途径。

Abstract: We propose a minimal quantum-mechanical model of a frequency dispersive, time-modulated medium that explicitly incorporates the pumping mechanism. It consists of a homogeneous ensemble of quantum emitters dressed by a pump laser. We analyze their dispersive, time-varying response to a perturbative probe field by deriving the macroscopic polarizability of the ensemble, and then solving Maxwell's equations via the Floquet formalism. This yields a rich band structure that departs from conventional analyses in which the modulation source is neglected, arising from the interplay between the dispersive polaritonic and time-varying responses. These results shed light on the role of the modulation source in time-varying media and pave the way toward realizing dispersive time-varying media from driven quantum-mechanical systems.

</details>


### [113] [Strong Epsilon-Near-Zero Confinement and Interface-Controlled Dynamics in Inverse-Designed Transparent Conducting Oxide Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24921)
*Jae Ik Choi,Ludmila J. Prokopeva,Vahagn Mkhitaryan,Yuheng Chen,Alexander V. Kildishev,Vladimir M. Shalaev,Alexandra Boltasseva*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作理论证明：将工作在ENZ点的TCO逆设计腔体与支持载流子输运的界面边界结合，可实现强且超快的光调制；逆设计框架配合结构无序感知修正回路，在真实材料无序下保持稳健共振吸收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间变化光子学的核心挑战是同时实现强光学调制与超快响应速度，传统TCO腔体几何在光场限制、电子温度调制深度与恢复速度上难以兼顾。

Method: 利用TCO在epsilon-near-zero点的ENZ增强吸收与电子加热效应，结合界面工程化的载流子弛豫；构建多层逆设计框架并加入结构无序感知修正回路，在超薄TCO薄膜中保持稳健共振吸收；通过界面势垒工程与邻层掺杂调控光谱与时间动力学。

Result: 相比常规TCO腔体几何，逆设计结构具有更强光场限制、更大电子温度调制和显著更快的恢复；逆设计腔体在几个光周期时间尺度上呈现更丰富的振荡透射动力学，界面控制可进一步实现时间压缩。

Conclusion: 光学限制与界面动力学的协同控制使逆设计TCO腔体成为支撑强、超快光学响应的时间变化光子介质可行平台。

Abstract: Achieving optical modulation that is both strong and ultrafast remains a central challenge in time-varying photonics. Here, we theoretically demonstrate that combining inverse-designed cavities that utilize transparent conducting oxides (TCOs) operating at the epsilon-near-zero (ENZ) point with boundaries that support carrier transport can meet this requirement. This is achieved by leveraging ENZ-enhanced optical absorption and electron heating with interface-engineered carrier relaxation. A multilayer inverse-design framework, combined with a structural disorder-aware correction loop, is developed to preserve robust resonant absorption in ultrathin TCO films under realistic material disorder. Relative to conventional TCO cavity geometries, the inverse-designed structures exhibit stronger light confinement, greater electron temperature modulation, and markedly faster recovery. Beyond cavity design, interfacial barrier engineering and adjacent-layer doping emerge as effective degrees of freedom for co-designing spectral and temporal dynamics. Furthermore, our theoretical framework shows that the inverse-designed cavity supports richer oscillatory transmittance dynamics on the few-optical-cycle timescale compared to its single-film counterpart, with interfacial control enabling further temporal compression. The combined control of optical confinement and interfacial dynamics establishes inverse-designed TCO cavities as a viable platform for time-varying photonic media capable of supporting strong and ultrafast optical responses.

</details>


### [114] [Residual Allan Stability under Periodic Auxiliary Ramsey Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24928)
*Zhiqian Su*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在固定名义 Fisher 信息率下，研究辅助 Ramsey 测量时序如何改变漂移脉冲频移所对应的频率稳定性。针对带因果预测的 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 漂移，推导更新周期平均的精确残差协方差与任意平均时间的 Allan 方差，并与连续 Kalman 参考对比；进一步推广到多模叠加、积分观测与零阶保持情形，并用三能级 CPT Ramsey 模型说明暗时间对测量调度选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定信息率（Fisher 信息）约束下，测量时序（schedule）本身会影响频率稳定度，而既有工作多关注恒定频移下的 Fisher 信息，缺少对漂移过程、周期采样与因果预测组合下频率稳定度（Allan 方差）的定量刻画，也缺少与连续 Kalman 最优参考的明确比较。

Method: 将漂移建模为 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程，考虑周期瞬时 Ramsey 观测并在更新间做因果预测；推导更新周期平均的精确残差协方差及任意平均时间下的 Allan 方差，并与同信息率的连续 Kalman 估计作对照。随后推广至有限个独立 OU 模的和、积分观测情形，并做零阶保持比较；最后建立三能级 CPT Ramsey 模型，将鉴别器响应映射为有效信息预算。

Result: 相对于同信息率的连续 Kalman 参考，周期估计增大周期平均的估计误差方差，降低短时残差 Allan 方差而增大其长时值；其协方差在原点附近衰减更慢且积分更大。多模情形下积分观测对标量漂移保持短时排序，数值计算给出交叉点对窗口时长的依赖。零阶保持比较表明，同一估计器驱动不同校正指令时短时排序可能改变。三能级 CPT 模型给出暗时间影响调度选择的示例。

Conclusion: 测量时序在固定信息率下会系统性改变漂移频移的频率稳定度：周期测量以牺牲平均估计精度换取更优短时 Allan 方差，但长时性能变差；该结论在多模、积分观测与不同校正指令下需按情形重新评估，暗时间等物理约束通过有效信息预算影响最优测量调度。

Abstract: The timing of auxiliary Ramsey measurements can change the frequency stability associated with a drifting pulse shift at a fixed nominal Fisher-information rate for a constant shift. For periodic instantaneous observations of an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck shift with causal prediction between updates, we derive the exact residual covariance averaged over the update cycle and the corresponding Allan variance at arbitrary averaging times. Relative to a continuous Kalman reference at the same information rate, periodic estimation increases the cycle-averaged estimation-error variance, reduces the short-time residual Allan variance, and increases its long-time value. Its covariance decays more slowly at the origin and has a larger integral. We extend the result to instantaneous sensing of the sum of finitely many independent Ornstein-Uhlenbeck modes. Integrated observations preserve the short-time ordering for the scalar shift, and numerical calculations resolve the dependence of the crossover on window duration. A zero-order-hold comparison shows that the short-time ordering can change when the same estimator drives a different correction command. A three-level Ramsey model of coherent population trapping (CPT) maps the discriminator response to an effective information budget and illustrates how the dark times affect the choice of schedule.

</details>
